````cpp
#include "core/ConfigManager.h"

#include <stdexcept>

#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

ConfigManager::ConfigManager(const std::string& dataDir) : dataDir_(dataDir) {
    loadBalance();
    loadGoods();
    loadLocations();
    loadNpcs();
    loadEndings();
    loadEvents();
    loadSocialPools();

    // ---- 交叉校验：引用完整性 ----
    for (const auto& g : goods_) {
        if (g.basePrice <= 0) fail("商品 " + g.id + " 基准价必须为正");
        if (g.slots <= 0) fail("商品 " + g.id + " 占格必须为正");
        if (g.limitPerDay <= 0) fail("商品 " + g.id + " 限购必须为正");
        if (g.soldAt.empty()) fail("商品 " + g.id + " 没有任何有售地点");
        for (const auto& loc : g.soldAt)
            if (!location(loc)) fail("商品 " + g.id + " 引用了不存在的地点 " + loc);
        for (const auto& [loc, f] : g.locFactor)
            if (!location(loc)) fail("商品 " + g.id + " 的价格系数引用了不存在的地点 " + loc);
    }
    for (const auto& n : npcs_)
        if (!location(n.home)) fail("NPC " + n.id + " 的驻地不存在: " + n.home);
    for (const auto& [loc, range] : balance_.jobs)
        if (!location(loc)) fail("balance.json jobs 引用了不存在的地点 " + loc);
    for (const auto& kv : balance_.jobNames)
        if (kv.first != "default" && !location(kv.first))
            fail("balance.json jobNames 引用了不存在的地点 " + kv.first);
    for (const auto& kv : balance_.jobStamina)
        if (kv.first != "default" && !location(kv.first))
            fail("balance.json jobStamina 引用了不存在的地点 " + kv.first);
    if (balance_.weeks <= 0) fail("周数必须为正");
    for (const auto& [npcId, pool] : socialPools_) {
        if (!npc(npcId)) fail("social.json 引用了不存在的 NPC " + npcId);
        if (pool.positive.empty() || pool.neutral.empty() || pool.negative.empty())
            fail("social.json NPC " + npcId + " 的选项池必须同时包含正/中/负三类");
    }
}

std::string ConfigManager::pathOf(const char* name) const {
    return dataDir_ + "/" + name;
}

[[noreturn]] void ConfigManager::fail(const std::string& what) const {
    throw std::runtime_error("[数据校验失败] " + what);
}

void ConfigManager::loadBalance() {
    Json j = ju::loadFile(pathOf("balance.json"));
    balance_.startMoney   = ju::getOr(j, "startMoney", 3000);
    balance_.weeklyExpense = ju::getOr(j, "weeklyExpense", 250);
    balance_.targetMoney  = ju::getOr(j, "targetMoney", 12000);
    balance_.weeks        = ju::getOr(j, "weeks", 18);
    balance_.teachingWeeks = ju::getOr(j, "teachingWeeks", 16);
    balance_.midtermWeek  = ju::getOr(j, "midtermWeek", 8);
    balance_.examStartWeek = ju::getOr(j, "examStartWeek", 17);
    balance_.examStudyBonus = ju::getOr(j, "examStudyBonus", 3);
    balance_.maxSlots     = ju::getOr(j, "maxSlots", 30);
    balance_.studyStart   = ju::getOr(j, "studyStart", 40);
    balance_.studyGain    = ju::getOr(j, "studyGain", 3);
    balance_.studyDecay   = ju::getOr(j, "studyDecay", 4);
    balance_.passScore    = ju::getOr(j, "passScore", 60);
    balance_.warnScore    = ju::getOr(j, "warnScore", 50);
    balance_.scholarshipScore = ju::getOr(j, "scholarshipScore", 85);
    balance_.scholarship  = ju::getOr(j, "scholarship", 1000);
    balance_.retakeFee    = ju::getOr(j, "retakeFee", 2000);
    balance_.eventChance  = ju::getOr(j, "eventChance", 0.22);
    balance_.rumorAccuracy = ju::getOr(j, "rumorAccuracy", 0.8);
    balance_.trendAmp     = ju::getOr(j, "trendAmp", 0.22);
    balance_.noiseAmp     = ju::getOr(j, "noiseAmp", 0.06);
    balance_.seasonAmp    = ju::getOr(j, "seasonAmp", 0.35);
    balance_.kaoyanDiscount   = ju::getOr(j, "kaoyanDiscount", 0.9);
    balance_.innerTicketBonus = ju::getOr(j, "innerTicketBonus", 1.25);
    balance_.seniorWorkBonus  = ju::getOr(j, "seniorWorkBonus", 1.2);

    // 炒股模拟器
    balance_.stockStartPrice = ju::getOr(j, "stockStartPrice", 10.0);
    balance_.stockVolMin     = ju::getOr(j, "stockVolMin", 0.01);
    balance_.stockVolMax     = ju::getOr(j, "stockVolMax", 0.06);
    balance_.stockFailProb   = ju::getOr(j, "stockFailProb", 0.62);
    balance_.stockCrashProb  = ju::getOr(j, "stockCrashProb", 0.03);
    balance_.stockCrashDrop  = ju::getOr(j, "stockCrashDrop", 0.25);
    balance_.stockBoomProb   = ju::getOr(j, "stockBoomProb", 0.02);
    balance_.stockBoomRise   = ju::getOr(j, "stockBoomRise", 0.30);
    balance_.stockFeeRate    = ju::getOr(j, "stockFeeRate", 0.005);
    balance_.stockLoanWeeklyRate = ju::getOr(j, "stockLoanWeeklyRate", 0.05);
    balance_.stockLeverages.clear();
    if (j.contains("stockLeverages") && j["stockLeverages"].is_array())
        for (const auto& l : j["stockLeverages"])
            balance_.stockLeverages.push_back(l.get<int>());
    if (balance_.stockLeverages.empty()) balance_.stockLeverages = {1, 2, 5};

    // 股力
    balance_.stockSkillStart     = ju::getOr(j, "stockSkillStart", 5);
    balance_.stockSkillMax       = ju::getOr(j, "stockSkillMax", 100);
    balance_.stockSkillFactor    = ju::getOr(j, "stockSkillFactor", 0.003);
    balance_.stockSkillFailFloor = ju::getOr(j, "stockSkillFailFloor", 0.25);
    balance_.learnStockStaminaCost = ju::getOr(j, "learnStockStaminaCost", 30);
    balance_.learnStockGainMin   = ju::getOr(j, "learnStockGainMin", 6);
    balance_.learnStockGainMax   = ju::getOr(j, "learnStockGainMax", 10);
    balance_.stockBarsKeep       = ju::getOr(j, "stockBarsKeep", 60);
    balance_.stockIntraSteps     = ju::getOr(j, "stockIntraSteps", 6);

    // 体力
    balance_.staminaMax           = ju::getOr(j, "staminaMax", 100);
    balance_.staminaStockCost     = ju::getOr(j, "staminaStockCost", 15);
    balance_.staminaLowThreshold  = ju::getOr(j, "staminaLowThreshold", 30);
    balance_.staminaLowFailBonus  = ju::getOr(j, "staminaLowFailBonus", 0.15);
    balance_.bankruptcyDebt       = ju::getOr(j, "bankruptcyDebt", 3000);

    // v2 时间系统：行动按"小时 + 体力"计费
    balance_.moveStamina        = ju::getOr(j, "moveStamina", 5);
    balance_.moveHours          = ju::getOr(j, "moveHours", 1);
    balance_.studyStamina       = ju::getOr(j, "studyStamina", 10);
    balance_.studyHours         = ju::getOr(j, "studyHours", 2);
    balance_.socialStamina      = ju::getOr(j, "socialStamina", 5);
    balance_.socialHours        = ju::getOr(j, "socialHours", 2);
    balance_.workHours          = ju::getOr(j, "workHours", 8);
    balance_.tradeHours         = ju::getOr(j, "tradeHours", 1);
    balance_.learnStockHours    = ju::getOr(j, "learnStockHours", 2);
    balance_.sleepFullHours     = ju::getOr(j, "sleepFullHours", 8);
    balance_.insomniaChance     = ju::getOr(j, "insomniaChance", 0.10);
    balance_.insomniaPenaltyHours = ju::getOr(j, "insomniaPenaltyHours", 2);
    balance_.faintHospitalFee     = ju::getOr(j, "faintHospitalFee", 50);
    balance_.faintMaxStaminaLoss  = ju::getOr(j, "faintMaxStaminaLoss", 10);

    if (j.contains("jobs") && j["jobs"].is_object()) {
        for (auto it = j["jobs"].begin(); it != j["jobs"].end(); ++it) {
            if (it.value().is_array() && it.value().size() >= 2)
                balance_.jobs[it.key()] = {it.value()[0].get<int>(), it.value()[1].get<int>()};
        }
    }
    if (j.contains("jobNames") && j["jobNames"].is_object())
        for (auto it = j["jobNames"].begin(); it != j["jobNames"].end(); ++it)
            balance_.jobNames[it.key()] = it.value().get<std::string>();
    if (j.contains("jobStamina") && j["jobStamina"].is_object())
        for (auto it = j["jobStamina"].begin(); it != j["jobStamina"].end(); ++it)
            balance_.jobStamina[it.key()] = it.value().get<int>();

    balance_.lowEnergyPayRate  = ju::getOr(j, "lowEnergyPayRate", 0.5);
    balance_.studyNightBonus   = ju::getOr(j, "studyNightBonus", 3);
    balance_.studyNightStamina = ju::getOr(j, "studyNightStamina", 15);
}

void ConfigManager::loadGoods() {
    Json j = ju::loadFile(pathOf("goods.json"));
    if (!j.is_array()) fail("goods.json 顶层必须是数组");
    for (const auto& e : j) {
        GoodsDef g;
        g.id         = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "id", "");
        g.name       = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "name", g.id);
        g.category   = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "category", "");
        g.basePrice  = ju::getOr(e, "basePrice", 1);
        g.spread     = ju::getOr(e, "spread", 0.08);
        g.slots      = ju::getOr(e, "slots", 1);
        g.limitPerDay = ju::getOr(e, "limitPerDay", 15);
        g.desc       = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "desc", "");
        if (g.id.empty()) fail("goods.json 存在缺少 id 的商品");
        if (e.contains("soldAt") && e["soldAt"].is_array())
            for (const auto& s : e["soldAt"]) g.soldAt.push_back(s.get<std::string>());
        if (e.contains("locFactor") && e["locFactor"].is_object())
            for (auto it = e["locFactor"].begin(); it != e["locFactor"].end(); ++it)
                g.locFactor[it.key()] = it.value().get<double>();
        if (e.contains("peakWeeks") && e["peakWeeks"].is_array())
            for (const auto& w : e["peakWeeks"]) g.peakWeeks.push_back(w.get<int>());
        goods_.push_back(std::move(g));
    }
    if (goods_.empty()) fail("goods.json 没有任何商品");
}

void ConfigManager::loadLocations() {
    Json j = ju::loadFile(pathOf("locations.json"));
    if (!j.is_array()) fail("locations.json 顶层必须是数组");
    for (const auto& e : j) {
        LocationDef l;
        l.id    = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "id", "");
        l.name  = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "name", l.id);
        l.desc  = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "desc", "");
        l.fare  = ju::getOr(e, "fare", 2);
        l.npcId = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "npcId", "");
        if (l.id.empty()) fail("locations.json 存在缺少 id 的地点");
        locations_.push_back(std::move(l));
    }
    if (locations_.empty()) fail("locations.json 没有任何地点");
}

void ConfigManager::loadNpcs() {
    Json j = ju::loadFile(pathOf("npcs.json"));
    if (!j.is_array()) return;
    for (const auto& e : j) {
        NpcDef n;
        n.id    = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "id", "");
        n.name  = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "name", n.id);
        n.home  = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "home", "");
        n.intro = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "intro", "");
        if (!n.id.empty()) npcs_.push_back(std::move(n));
    }
}

void ConfigManager::loadEndings() {
    Json j = ju::loadFile(pathOf("endings.json"));
    if (!j.is_array()) fail("endings.json 顶层必须是数组");
    for (const auto& e : j) {
        EndingDef d;
        d.id       = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "id", "");
        d.title    = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "title", d.id);
        d.text     = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "text", "");
        d.priority = ju::getOr(e, "priority", 99);
        if (e.contains("conditions") && e["conditions"].is_array())
            for (const auto& c : e["conditions"]) d.conditions.push_back(c);
        if (!d.id.empty()) endings_.push_back(std::move(d));
    }
    if (endings_.empty()) fail("endings.json 至少需要一个结局");
}

void ConfigManager::loadEvents() {
    rawEvents_ = ju::loadFile(pathOf("events.json"));
    if (!rawEvents_.is_array()) fail("events.json 顶层必须是数组");
}

void ConfigManager::loadSocialPools() {
    Json j = ju::loadFile(pathOf("social.json"));
    if (!j.is_object()) fail("social.json 顶层必须是对象（npcId → 池）");
    for (auto it = j.begin(); it != j.end(); ++it) {
        SocialPoolDef pool;
        const Json& p = it.value();
        if (p.contains("positive") && p["positive"].is_array())
            for (const auto& e : p["positive"]) pool.positive.push_back(e.get<std::string>());
        if (p.contains("neutral") && p["neutral"].is_array())
            for (const auto& e : p["neutral"]) pool.neutral.push_back(e.get<std::string>());
        if (p.contains("negative") && p["negative"].is_array())
            for (const auto& e : p["negative"]) pool.negative.push_back(e.get<std::string>());
        socialPools_[it.key()] = std::move(pool);
    }
}

const SocialPoolDef* ConfigManager::socialPool(const std::string& npcId) const {
    auto it = socialPools_.find(npcId);
    return it == socialPools_.end() ? nullptr : &it->second;
}

const GoodsDef* ConfigManager::goods(const std::string& id) const {
    for (const auto& g : goods_)
        if (g.id == id) return &g;
    return nullptr;
}

const LocationDef* ConfigManager::location(const std::string& id) const {
    for (const auto& l : locations_)
        if (l.id == id) return &l;
    return nullptr;
}

const NpcDef* ConfigManager::npc(const std::string& id) const {
    for (const auto& n : npcs_)
        if (n.id == id) return &n;
    return nullptr;
}
````

## `src\core\Game.cpp`

````cpp
#include "core/Game.h"

#include <iostream>

#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "core/ITimeListener.h"
#include "core/Session.h"
#include "economy/Market.h"
#include "events/EndingJudge.h"
#include "events/EventScheduler.h"
#include "events/GameEvent.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "stock/StockSystem.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"
#include "util/StringUtils.h"
#include "ui/Screens.h"

Game::Game(const std::string& dataDir, const std::string& saveDir, std::uint64_t seed)
    : config_(dataDir), rng_(seed), saves_(saveDir) {}

Game::~Game() = default;

std::string Game::readLine() {
    std::string line;
    if (!std::getline(std::cin, line)) {
        running_ = false; // EOF：干净退出
        return "";
    }
    return strutil::trim(line);
}

void Game::pushScreen(std::unique_ptr<IScreen> screen) {
    screens_.push(std::move(screen));
}

void Game::resolveFlow() {
    screens_.flushGraveyard(); // 销毁上一次输入处理中弹出的屏幕
    if (!ctx_) {
        if (screens_.empty()) pushScreen(std::make_unique<TitleScreen>(*this));
        return;
    }
    // 待弹窗（周报/事件）依序入栈；模态弹窗显示期间等待
    while (!pendingDialogs_.empty()) {
        if (screens_.topIsModal()) break;
        pushScreen(std::move(pendingDialogs_.front()));
        pendingDialogs_.pop_front();
    }
    if (screens_.empty()) {
        if (endingPending_) {
            endingPending_ = false;
            pushScreen(std::make_unique<EndingScreen>(*this));
        } else {
            pushScreen(std::make_unique<CampusScreen>(*this));
        }
    }
}

void Game::run() {
    while (running_) {
        resolveFlow();
        IScreen* top = screens_.top();
        if (!top) break;
        top->render();
        std::string input = readLine();
        if (!running_) break;
        top->handleInput(input);
    }
}

// ---------------- 流程控制 ----------------

void Game::newGame() {
    ctx_ = std::make_unique<GameContext>(config_, rng_, bus_);
    ctx_->initNewGame();
    screens_.popAll();
    pendingDialogs_.clear();
    endingPending_ = false;
    chosenEnding_ = nullptr;
    ctx_->lastFeedback = "开学了。启动资金 ¥" + std::to_string(config_.balance().startMoney) +
                         "，这学期不再向家里要钱。";
    // 第 1 周固定节点（开学事件）入队
    for (const GameEvent* ev : ctx_->events->fixedForWeek(1, *ctx_)) queueEventDialog(ev);
}

bool Game::loadGame(int slot, std::string& err) {
    Json data = saves_.load(slot, &err);
    if (data.is_null()) return false;
    try {
        if (!ctx_) ctx_ = std::make_unique<GameContext>(config_, rng_, bus_);
        ctx_->loadFromJson(data);
        if (data.contains("rng")) rng_.fromJson(data["rng"]);
        screens_.popAll();
        pendingDialogs_.clear();
        endingPending_ = false;
        chosenEnding_ = nullptr;
        return true;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        err = std::string("读档失败: ") + e.what();
        return false;
    }
}

bool Game::saveGame(int slot, std::string& err) {
    if (!ctx_) {
        err = "当前没有进行中的游戏";
        return false;
    }
    Json data = ctx_->toJson();
    data["version"] = 1;
    data["rng"] = rng_.toJson();
    data["meta"]["week"] = ctx_->time->week();
    data["meta"]["money"] = ctx_->player->wallet().get();
    return saves_.save(slot, data, &err);
}

void Game::performAction(std::unique_ptr<ICommand> cmd) {
    GameContext* c = ctx_.get();
    if (!c || !cmd) return;
    ActionOutcome out = Session::runAction(*c, *cmd);
    c->lastFeedback = out.result.msg;
    if (out.event) queueEventDialog(out.event);
    checkBankruptcy(); // 行动中的股价波动可能触发爆仓欠债
    // 跨越周日 → 自动进入周结算
    if (out.weekAdvanced && !c->time->isSemesterOver()) {
        requestEndWeek();
    }
}

// 炒股爆仓欠债超过破产线 → 立即终止本学期，进入《破产清算》结局
void Game::checkBankruptcy() {
    GameContext* c = ctx_.get();
    if (!c || endingPending_ || chosenEnding_) return;
    if (c->flags->has("bankrupt") && !c->time->isSemesterOver()) {
        chosenEnding_ = EndingJudge::judge(*c); // priority=0 的破产结局优先生效
        EndingDecidedEvent e{chosenEnding_->id, chosenEnding_->title};
        bus().publish(e);
        endingPending_ = true;
        pendingDialogs_.clear();
        screens_.popAll(); // 当前界面全部退役，resolveFlow 会推入结局屏
    }
}

// [8] 快进到周日结算：剩余天数逐日睡过（体力回满），然后进入周结算
void Game::finishWeekNow() {
    GameContext* c = ctx_.get();
    if (!c || endingPending_ || c->time->isSemesterOver()) return;
    while (true) {
        const bool weekRolled = c->time->advanceDay();
        c->player->energy().restoreFull(); // 睡满一整天
        ITimeListener& marketListener = *c->market;
        marketListener.onDayTick();
        c->stock->onTick(*c); // 跳过的天 K 线也照走
        if (weekRolled || c->time->isSemesterOver()) break;
    }
    checkBankruptcy();
    requestEndWeek();
}

void Game::requestEndWeek() {
    GameContext* c = ctx_.get();
    if (!c || endingPending_) return;

    WeekSettlement settle(*c);
    lastReport_ = settle.run();

    // 每周日自动存档（分工 7.2）
    std::string err;
    saveGame(SaveManager::kAutoSlot, err);

    if (c->flags->has("bankrupt")) {
        // 本周爆仓欠债超过破产线：先看结算报告，关闭后直接进破产结局
        chosenEnding_ = EndingJudge::judge(*c);
        bus().publish(EndingDecidedEvent{chosenEnding_->id, chosenEnding_->title});
        endingPending_ = true;
        queueWeeklyReport();
        return;
    }

    queueWeeklyReport(); // 周报最先弹出
    if (c->time->isSemesterOver()) {
        chosenEnding_ = EndingJudge::judge(*c);
        bus().publish(EndingDecidedEvent{chosenEnding_->id, chosenEnding_->title});
        endingPending_ = true;
        screens_.popAll(); // 校园退役，周报关闭后直接进结局
    } else {
        // 新一周的固定节点（期中/双十一/期末…）依序入队
        for (const GameEvent* ev : c->events->fixedForWeek(c->time->week(), *c))
            queueEventDialog(ev);
    }
}

void Game::backToTitle() {
    ctx_.reset();
    pendingDialogs_.clear();
    endingPending_ = false;
    chosenEnding_ = nullptr;
    screens_.popAll();
}

void Game::queueEventDialog(const GameEvent* ev) {
    if (ev) pendingDialogs_.push_back(std::make_unique<EventDialogScreen>(*this, ev));
}

void Game::queueWeeklyReport() {
    pendingDialogs_.push_back(std::make_unique<WeeklyReportScreen>(*this));
}
````

## `src\core\GameContext.cpp`

````cpp
#include "core/GameContext.h"

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/DomainEvents.h"
#include "core/EventBus.h"
#include "core/Random.h"
#include "economy/Market.h"
#include "events/EventScheduler.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "player/TradeSystem.h"
#include "story/NpcSystem.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "stock/StockSystem.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"
#include "core/ITimeListener.h"

GameContext::GameContext(const ConfigManager& config, Random& rng, EventBus& bus)
    : config(config), rng(rng), bus(bus) {
    // ---- 组合根：所有系统在这里组装（分工 7.2 GameContext 职责） ----
    player = std::make_unique<Player>(config);
    market = std::make_unique<Market>(config, rng);
    time = std::make_unique<TimeSystem>(config);
    events = std::make_unique<EventScheduler>(config, rng);
    flags = std::make_unique<StoryFlags>();
    npcs = std::make_unique<NpcSystem>(config);
    trade = std::make_unique<TradeSystem>(config);
    stock = std::make_unique<StockSystem>(config, rng);

    events->buildFromConfig(); // 事件表随配置构建（新局/读档共用）

    wireSubscriptions();
}

GameContext::~GameContext() {
    // 摘掉本上下文注册的订阅：Player 随上下文析构，留着订阅就是悬垂指针
    for (const std::uint64_t id : subscriptionIds_) bus.unsubscribe(id);
    subscriptionIds_.clear();
}

// 破产判定：钱包被炒股债务扣到破产线以下
bool GameContext::isBankrupt() const {
    return player->wallet().get() < -config.balance().bankruptcyDebt;
}

void GameContext::updateBankruptcyFlag() {
    if (isBankrupt()) flags->set("bankrupt");
}

// 昏迷/宵禁统一入口：任何行动（含屏幕上的免费交易）把时间推过凌晨 2 点都会走到这里
bool GameContext::handleFaintAndAdvance(std::string& appendMsg) {
    if (time->hour() < TimeSystem::kCurfewHour) return false;

    const BalanceDef& b = config.balance();
    const int fee = std::min(player->wallet().get(), b.faintHospitalFee);
    if (fee > 0) player->wallet().spend(fee);
    player->energy().reduceMax(b.faintMaxStaminaLoss);
    const bool weekAdvanced = time->advanceDay();
    locationId = "dorm"; // 被室友抬回宿舍
    ITimeListener& marketListener = *market;
    marketListener.onDayTick(); // 新的一天：限购/噪声刷新
    appendMsg += "\n【昏迷】凌晨 2 点，你眼前一黑倒在原地……醒来时已躺在校医院"
                 "（诊疗费 -¥" + std::to_string(fee) +
                 "，体力上限 -" + std::to_string(b.faintMaxStaminaLoss) + "）";
    updateBankruptcyFlag();
    return weekAdvanced;
}

// Observer 接线：交易完成 → 玩家自动记账（订阅方只依赖事件，不依赖发布方）
void GameContext::wireSubscriptions() {
    Player* p = player.get();
    subscriptionIds_.push_back(
        bus.subscribe<TradeCompletedEvent>([p](const TradeCompletedEvent& e) {
            p->onTradeCompleted(e);
        }));
}

void GameContext::initNewGame() {
    player->wallet().add(config.balance().startMoney);
    market->syncWeek(1);
    market->onWeekStart(); // 第 1 周趋势
    market->rollRumor();   // 生成对第 2 周的情报
    rumors = market->rumorTexts();
    bus.publish(WeekStartedEvent{1});
}

Json GameContext::toJson() const {
    return Json{
        {"meta", Json{{"location", locationId}, {"rumors", rumors}}},
        {"player", player->toJson()},
        {"market", market->toJson()},
        {"stock", stock->toJson()},
        {"time", time->toJson()},
        {"events", events->toJson()},
        {"flags", flags->toJson()},
        {"npcs", npcs->toJson()},
    };
}

void GameContext::loadFromJson(const Json& save) {
    if (save.contains("meta")) {
        locationId = ju::getOr<std::string>(save["meta"], "location", locationId);
        if (!config.location(locationId)) locationId = "dorm"; // 坏档兜底
        rumors.clear();
        if (save["meta"].contains("rumors") && save["meta"]["rumors"].is_array())
            for (const auto& r : save["meta"]["rumors"]) rumors.push_back(r.get<std::string>());
    }
    if (save.contains("player")) player->fromJson(save["player"]);
    if (save.contains("market")) market->fromJson(save["market"]);
    if (save.contains("stock")) stock->fromJson(save["stock"]);
    if (save.contains("time")) time->fromJson(save["time"]);
    if (save.contains("events")) events->fromJson(save["events"]);
    if (save.contains("flags")) flags->fromJson(save["flags"]);
    if (save.contains("npcs")) npcs->fromJson(save["npcs"]);
}
````

## `src\core\Random.cpp`

````cpp
#include "core/Random.h"

#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

std::uint64_t Random::next() {
    std::uint64_t z = (state_ += 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ull);
    z = (z ^ (z >> 30)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9ull;
    z = (z ^ (z >> 27)) * 0x94D049BB133111EBull;
    return z ^ (z >> 31);
}

int Random::range(int lo, int hi) {
    if (hi <= lo) return lo;
    return lo + static_cast<int>(next() % static_cast<std::uint64_t>(hi - lo + 1));
}

double Random::real() {
    return static_cast<double>(next() >> 11) * (1.0 / 9007199254740992.0);
}

bool Random::chance(double p) {
    if (p >= 1.0) return true;
    if (p <= 0.0) return false;
    return real() < p;
}

double Random::uniform(double lo, double hi) {
    return lo + real() * (hi - lo);
}

Json Random::toJson() const {
    return Json{{"state", state_}};
}

void Random::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    state_ = ju::getOr(j, "state", state_);
}
````

## `src\core\ScreenManager.cpp`

````cpp
#include "core/ScreenManager.h"

void ScreenManager::push(std::unique_ptr<IScreen> screen) {
    if (!screen) return;
    graveyard_.clear(); // 上一次输入处理已结束，旧屏幕可以销毁了
    IScreen* raw = screen.get();
    stack_.push_back(std::move(screen));
    raw->onEnter();
}

void ScreenManager::pop() {
    if (stack_.empty()) return;
    graveyard_.push_back(std::move(stack_.back())); // 延迟销毁：等下一次 push 再释放
    stack_.pop_back();
}

void ScreenManager::popAll() {
    while (!stack_.empty()) pop();
}

void ScreenManager::flushGraveyard() {
    graveyard_.clear();
}
````

## `src\core\Session.cpp`

````cpp
#include "core/Session.h"

#include <algorithm>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/DomainEvents.h"
#include "core/EventBus.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "core/IEffect.h"
#include "core/ITimeListener.h"
#include "core/Random.h"
#include "economy/Market.h"
#include "events/GameEvent.h"
#include "events/EventScheduler.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "stock/StockSystem.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"

ActionOutcome Session::runAction(GameContext& ctx, const ICommand& cmd) {
    ActionOutcome out;
    if (!ctx.time->canAct()) {
        out.result = {false, false, "凌晨 2 点的困意压垮了你……（昏迷边缘，先睡觉）"};
        return out;
    }
    const int weekBefore = ctx.time->week();
    const int dayBefore = ctx.time->day();

    ActionResult r = cmd.execute(ctx); // 命令内部扣除体力与小时数
    out.result = r;
    if (!(r.ok && r.consumedTime)) return out;

    // 股价随每次行动走一根 K 线（持仓爆仓会在此触发）
    ctx.stock->onTick(ctx);
    ctx.bus.publish(ActionTakenEvent{cmd.name()});

    // ---- 昏迷判定：熬过凌晨 2 点（hour >= 26）→ 统一入口处理 ----
    bool dayChanged = false;
    {
        std::string faintMsg;
        out.weekAdvanced = ctx.handleFaintAndAdvance(faintMsg);
        if (!faintMsg.empty()) {
            r.msg += faintMsg;
            out.result.msg = r.msg;
            out.fainted = true;
            dayChanged = true;
        }
    }

    // ---- 跨天（睡觉/昏迷推进）：行情按天刷新 ----
    if (ctx.time->day() != dayBefore || ctx.time->week() != weekBefore) dayChanged = true;
    if (dayChanged) {
        ITimeListener& marketListener = *ctx.market;
        marketListener.onDayTick(); // 限购/噪声按天刷新
    }
    if (ctx.time->week() != weekBefore) out.weekAdvanced = true;

    // 概率摇随机事件（数据驱动，分工 8.2）
    if (ctx.rng.chance(ctx.config.balance().eventChance)) {
        if (const GameEvent* ev = ctx.events->rollRandom(ctx)) out.event = ev;
    }
    return out;
}

std::string Session::applyChoice(GameContext& ctx, const GameEvent& ev, int choiceIdx) {
    if (ev.choices.empty()) return "";
    if (choiceIdx < 0 || choiceIdx >= static_cast<int>(ev.choices.size())) choiceIdx = 0;
    const auto& choice = ev.choices[static_cast<std::size_t>(choiceIdx)];

    std::string out = "你选择了：" + choice.text;
    for (const auto& effect : choice.effects) {
        std::string msg = effect->apply(ctx);
        if (!msg.empty()) out += "\n  · " + msg;
    }
    ctx.flags->set("evt_done_" + ev.id); // 一次性事件标记（GameEvent::canTrigger 读取）
    ctx.events->markTriggered(ev);       // 随机事件冷却登记
    return out;
}
````

## `src\core\WeekSettlement.cpp`

````cpp
#include "core/WeekSettlement.h"

#include <algorithm>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/DomainEvents.h"
#include "core/EventBus.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "economy/Market.h"
#include "events/EventScheduler.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "player/StudySystem.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "stock/StockSystem.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

WeekSettlement::Report WeekSettlement::run() {
    rep_ = Report{};
    rep_.week = ctx_.time->settledWeek(); // 周翻转后结算：结算的是刚结束的那一周
    rep_.moneyBefore = ctx_.player->wallet().get();

    chargeExpenses();
    settleStudy();
    applyScheduledEffects();
    settleStocks();
    settleEnergy();
    checkMilestones();
    beginNewWeek();
    refreshMarketAndRumors();

    rep_.moneyAfter = ctx_.player->wallet().get();
    rep_.semesterOver = ctx_.time->isSemesterOver();
    return rep_;
}

void WeekSettlement::chargeExpenses() {
    int expense = std::min(ctx_.config.balance().weeklyExpense, ctx_.player->wallet().get());
    if (expense > 0) ctx_.player->wallet().spend(expense);
    rep_.lines.push_back("生活费支出 -¥" + std::to_string(expense) +
                         "（余额 ¥" + std::to_string(ctx_.player->wallet().get()) + "）");
}

void WeekSettlement::settleStudy() {
    StudyWeekOutcome o = ctx_.player->study().onWeekEnd(
        ctx_.time->settledWeek(), ctx_.time->lastWeekStudied());

    if (o.decay > 0)
        rep_.lines.push_back("这周一次都没学习，平时分 -" + std::to_string(o.decay) +
                             "（当前 " + std::to_string(ctx_.player->studyScore()) + "）");

    if (o.midtermWarn)
        rep_.lines.push_back("【期中警告】平时分 " +
                             std::to_string(ctx_.player->studyScore()) +
                             " 低于警戒线 " +
                             std::to_string(ctx_.config.balance().warnScore) +
                             "，期末再不冲刺要挂科了！");

    if (o.failedNow) {
        ctx_.flags->set("failed");
        const int fee = std::min(o.retakeFee, ctx_.player->wallet().get());
        if (fee > 0) ctx_.player->wallet().spend(fee);
        rep_.lines.push_back("【期末】平时分 " +
                             std::to_string(ctx_.player->studyScore()) +
                             "，挂科了……补考费 -¥" + std::to_string(fee));
    } else if (ctx_.time->settledWeek() == ctx_.config.balance().weeks) {
        rep_.lines.push_back("【期末】平时分 " +
                             std::to_string(ctx_.player->studyScore()) + "，稳过！");
        if (o.scholarship > 0) {
            ctx_.player->wallet().add(o.scholarship);
            rep_.lines.push_back("【奖学金】平时分 ≥" +
                                 std::to_string(ctx_.config.balance().scholarshipScore) +
                                 "，获得奖学金 +¥" + std::to_string(o.scholarship));
        }
    }
}

void WeekSettlement::applyScheduledEffects() {
    // 延迟效果到期的本周生效（如借室友的钱被归还）
    ctx_.events->applyDueEffects(ctx_);
}

void WeekSettlement::settleStocks() {
    // 融资负债按周计息（先计息再走价格，爆仓在此触发）
    long long interest = ctx_.stock->chargeWeeklyInterest();
    if (interest > 0)
        rep_.lines.push_back("【股市】融资利息 -¥" + std::to_string(interest) +
                             "（负债按周计息）");
    ctx_.stock->onTick(ctx_); // 周日股价也波动一格
}

void WeekSettlement::settleEnergy() {
    // 昏迷造成的体力上限扣减在周结算时恢复；睡眠回体力由睡觉机制负责
    ctx_.player->energy().restoreMax(ctx_.config.balance().staminaMax);
}

void WeekSettlement::checkMilestones() {
    // 期中警告/期末判定/奖学金/补考费已并入 settleStudy（C，W4）
}

void WeekSettlement::beginNewWeek() {
    // 周数已在 TimeSystem::advanceDay 中翻转；这里只做冷却递减与广播
    ctx_.events->onWeekStart(); // 事件冷却递减
    ctx_.bus.publish(WeekStartedEvent{ctx_.time->week()});
}

void WeekSettlement::refreshMarketAndRumors() {
    int newWeek = ctx_.time->week();
    ctx_.market->syncWeek(newWeek);
    ctx_.market->onWeekStart();   // 新一周趋势（消化上周的情报）
    ctx_.market->rollRumor();     // 摇下周的新情报
    ctx_.rumors = ctx_.market->rumorTexts();
    if (!ctx_.rumors.empty())
        rep_.lines.push_back(ctx_.rumors.front());
}
````

## `src\economy\Market.cpp`

````cpp
#include "economy/Market.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/Random.h"
#include "economy/PriceModels.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

Market::Market(const ConfigManager& cfg, Random& rng)
    : cfg_(cfg), rng_(rng), chain_(buildDefaultPriceChain(cfg.balance())) {}

Market::~Market() = default;

double Market::trendOf(const std::string& locId, const std::string& goodsId) const {
    auto it = trend_.find(locId);
    if (it == trend_.end()) return 0.0;
    auto it2 = it->second.find(goodsId);
    return it2 == it->second.end() ? 0.0 : it2->second;
}

double Market::noiseOf(const std::string& goodsId) const {
    auto it = noiseMult_.find(goodsId);
    return it == noiseMult_.end() ? 1.0 : it->second;
}

double Market::shockOf(const std::string& goodsId) const {
    auto it = shock_.find(goodsId);
    return it == shock_.end() ? 1.0 : it->second.first;
}

double Market::midPrice(const std::string& locId, const std::string& goodsId) const {
    const GoodsDef* g = cfg_.goods(goodsId);
    const LocationDef* l = cfg_.location(locId);
    if (!g || !l) return 1.0;
    PriceContext c{*g, *l, week_, trendOf(locId, goodsId), noiseOf(goodsId), shockOf(goodsId)};
    return chain_->price(c);
}

int Market::buyPrice(const std::string& locId, const std::string& goodsId) const {
    const GoodsDef* g = cfg_.goods(goodsId);
    if (!g) return 1;
    double mid = midPrice(locId, goodsId);
    return std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::round(mid * (1.0 + g->spread))));
}

int Market::sellPrice(const std::string& locId, const std::string& goodsId) const {
    const GoodsDef* g = cfg_.goods(goodsId);
    if (!g) return 1;
    double mid = midPrice(locId, goodsId);
    return std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::round(mid * (1.0 - g->spread))));
}

std::vector<Market::Listing> Market::listAt(const std::string& locId) const {
    std::vector<Listing> out;
    out.reserve(cfg_.goods().size());
    for (const auto& g : cfg_.goods()) {
        Listing li;
        li.goods = &g;
        li.buyPrice = buyPrice(locId, g.id);
        li.sellPrice = sellPrice(locId, g.id);
        li.canBuy = std::find(g.soldAt.begin(), g.soldAt.end(), locId) != g.soldAt.end();
        li.quotaLeft = quotaLeft(locId, g.id);
        out.push_back(li);
    }
    return out;
}

int Market::quotaLeft(const std::string& locId, const std::string& goodsId) const {
    auto it = quota_.find(locId);
    if (it == quota_.end()) {
        const GoodsDef* g = cfg_.goods(goodsId);
        return g ? g->limitPerDay : 0;
    }
    auto it2 = it->second.find(goodsId);
    if (it2 != it->second.end()) return it2->second;
    const GoodsDef* g = cfg_.goods(goodsId);
    return g ? g->limitPerDay : 0;
}

bool Market::consumeQuota(const std::string& locId, const std::string& goodsId, int qty) {
    if (quotaLeft(locId, goodsId) < qty) return false;
    quota_[locId][goodsId] -= qty;
    return true;
}

void Market::applyShock(const std::string& goodsId, double mult, int days) {
    if (!cfg_.goods(goodsId)) return;
    if (mult <= 0.0) mult = 1.0;
    if (days <= 0) days = 1;
    shock_[goodsId] = {mult, days};
}

void Market::onDayTick() {
    double amp = cfg_.balance().noiseAmp;
    for (const auto& g : cfg_.goods())
        noiseMult_[g.id] = 1.0 + rng_.uniform(-amp, amp);
    // 冲击衰减：按"行动点/天"倒数
    for (auto it = shock_.begin(); it != shock_.end();) {
        if (--it->second.second <= 0) it = shock_.erase(it);
        else ++it;
    }
    resetQuotas(); // 限购按"次到访/天"刷新（分工 3.4：每地点每日 10-20 件）
}

void Market::resetQuotas() {
    quota_.clear();
    for (const auto& g : cfg_.goods())
        for (const auto& loc : g.soldAt)
            quota_[loc][g.id] = g.limitPerDay;
}

void Market::onWeekStart() {
    double amp = cfg_.balance().trendAmp;
    // 全部 (loc, goods) 摇新趋势；情报命中的商品按情报方向偏移
    for (const auto& l : cfg_.locations()) {
        for (const auto& g : cfg_.goods()) {
            double t = rng_.uniform(-amp, amp);
            if (hasRumor_ && pendingRumor_.goodsId == g.id) {
                bool hit = rng_.chance(cfg_.balance().rumorAccuracy);
                double strength = rng_.uniform(0.4, 1.0) * amp;
                t = hit ? (pendingRumor_.up ? strength : -strength)
                        : rng_.uniform(-amp, amp);
            }
            trend_[l.id][g.id] = t;
        }
    }
    hasRumor_ = false;
    onDayTick(); // 新一周的第一天：噪声 + 限购
}

void Market::rollRumor() {
    const auto& goods = cfg_.goods();
    const GoodsDef* g = &goods[rng_.next() % goods.size()];
    bool up = rng_.chance(0.5);
    pendingRumor_ = {g->id, up};
    hasRumor_ = true;
    rumorTexts_.clear();
    rumorTexts_.push_back(std::string("【室友情报】听说下周「") + g->name + "」的价格可能要" +
                          (up ? "涨" : "跌") + "，准确率约 " +
                          std::to_string(static_cast<int>(cfg_.balance().rumorAccuracy * 100)) + "%");
}

const char* Market::trendArrow(const std::string& locId, const std::string& goodsId) const {
    double t = trendOf(locId, goodsId);
    if (t > 0.06) return "▲";
    if (t < -0.06) return "▼";
    return "─";
}

Json Market::toJson() const {
    Json trends = Json::object();
    for (const auto& [loc, gm] : trend_) {
        Json o = Json::object();
        for (const auto& [g, t] : gm) o[g] = t;
        trends[loc] = o;
    }
    Json noise = Json::object();
    for (const auto& [g, m] : noiseMult_) noise[g] = m;
    Json shocks = Json::array();
    for (const auto& [g, sd] : shock_)
        shocks.push_back(Json{{"goods", g}, {"mult", sd.first}, {"days", sd.second}});
    Json quotas = Json::object();
    for (const auto& [loc, gm] : quota_) {
        Json o = Json::object();
        for (const auto& [g, q] : gm) o[g] = q;
        quotas[loc] = o;
    }
    Json rumor;
    if (hasRumor_) rumor = Json{{"goods", pendingRumor_.goodsId}, {"up", pendingRumor_.up}};
    return Json{
        {"week", week_},
        {"trend", trends},
        {"noise", noise},
        {"shock", shocks},
        {"quota", quotas},
        {"pendingRumor", rumor},
        {"rumorTexts", rumorTexts_},
    };
}

void Market::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    week_ = ju::getOr(j, "week", week_);
    trend_.clear();
    if (j.contains("trend") && j["trend"].is_object())
        for (auto it = j["trend"].begin(); it != j["trend"].end(); ++it)
            for (auto it2 = it.value().begin(); it2 != it.value().end(); ++it2)
                trend_[it.key()][it2.key()] = it2.value().get<double>();
    noiseMult_.clear();
    if (j.contains("noise") && j["noise"].is_object())
        for (auto it = j["noise"].begin(); it != j["noise"].end(); ++it)
            noiseMult_[it.key()] = it.value().get<double>();
    shock_.clear();
    if (j.contains("shock") && j["shock"].is_array())
        for (const auto& e : j["shock"]) {
            std::string g = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "goods", "");
            if (!cfg_.goods(g)) continue;
            shock_[g] = {ju::getOr(e, "mult", 1.0), ju::getOr(e, "days", 1)};
        }
    quota_.clear();
    if (j.contains("quota") && j["quota"].is_object())
        for (auto it = j["quota"].begin(); it != j["quota"].end(); ++it)
            for (auto it2 = it.value().begin(); it2 != it.value().end(); ++it2)
                quota_[it.key()][it2.key()] = it2.value().get<int>();
    hasRumor_ = false;
    if (j.contains("pendingRumor") && j["pendingRumor"].is_object()) {
        std::string g = ju::getOr<std::string>(j["pendingRumor"], "goods", "");
        if (cfg_.goods(g)) {
            pendingRumor_ = {g, ju::getOr(j["pendingRumor"], "up", true)};
            hasRumor_ = true;
        }
    }
    rumorTexts_.clear();
    if (j.contains("rumorTexts") && j["rumorTexts"].is_array())
        for (const auto& t : j["rumorTexts"]) rumorTexts_.push_back(t.get<std::string>());
}
````

## `src\economy\PriceModels.cpp`

````cpp
#include "economy/PriceModels.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

double BasePriceModel::price(const PriceContext& c) const {
    double locFactor = 1.0;
    auto it = c.goods.locFactor.find(c.location.id);
    if (it != c.goods.locFactor.end()) locFactor = it->second;
    return static_cast<double>(c.goods.basePrice) * locFactor;
}

double SeasonDecorator::factor(const PriceContext& c) const {
    for (int w : c.goods.peakWeeks)
        if (w == c.week) return 1.0 + amp_;
    return 1.0;
}

double TrendDecorator::factor(const PriceContext& c) const {
    return 1.0 + c.trend;
}

double ShockDecorator::factor(const PriceContext& c) const {
    return c.shockMult;
}

double NoiseDecorator::factor(const PriceContext& c) const {
    return c.noiseMult;
}

double ClampDecorator::price(const PriceContext& c) const {
    double p = inner_->price(c);
    p = std::clamp(p, 1.0, 999999.0);
    return std::round(p);
}

std::unique_ptr<IPriceModel> buildDefaultPriceChain(const BalanceDef& b) {
    // 组装顺序：base → season → trend → shock → noise → clamp
    std::unique_ptr<IPriceModel> chain = std::make_unique<BasePriceModel>();
    chain = std::make_unique<SeasonDecorator>(std::move(chain), b.seasonAmp);
    chain = std::make_unique<TrendDecorator>(std::move(chain));
    chain = std::make_unique<ShockDecorator>(std::move(chain));
    chain = std::make_unique<NoiseDecorator>(std::move(chain));
    chain = std::make_unique<ClampDecorator>(std::move(chain));
    return chain;
}
````

## `src\events\Condition.cpp`

````cpp
#include "events/Condition.h"

#include <stdexcept>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "player/TradeSystem.h"
#include "story/NpcSystem.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

bool WeekBetween::test(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    int w = ctx.time->week();
    return w >= min_ && w <= max_;
}

bool LocationIs::test(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    return ctx.locationId == locId_;
}

bool MinMoney::test(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    return ctx.player->wallet().get() >= v_;
}

bool TotalAssetsMin::test(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    return ctx.player->wallet().get() + ctx.trade->estimatePortfolioValue(ctx) >= v_;
}

bool HasFlag::test(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    // "not:xxx" 前缀 = 取反（结局条件里用：not:failed 表示"没挂科"）
    if (flag_.size() > 4 && flag_.compare(0, 4, "not:") == 0)
        return !ctx.flags->has(flag_.substr(4));
    return ctx.flags->has(flag_);
}

bool AffinityMin::test(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    return ctx.npcs->atLeast(npcId_, v_);
}

bool AffinityAnyMin::test(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    return ctx.npcs->maxAffinity() >= v_;
}

bool InventoryNotEmpty::test(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    return !ctx.player->inventory().empty();
}

std::unique_ptr<ICondition> ConditionFactory::create(const Json& j) {
    std::string type = ju::getOr<std::string>(j, "type", "");
    if (type == "weekBetween")
        return std::make_unique<WeekBetween>(ju::getOr(j, "min", 1), ju::getOr(j, "max", 12));
    if (type == "locationIs")
        return std::make_unique<LocationIs>(ju::getOr<std::string>(j, "location", ""));
    if (type == "minMoney")
        return std::make_unique<MinMoney>(ju::getOr(j, "value", 0));
    if (type == "totalAssetsMin")
        return std::make_unique<TotalAssetsMin>(ju::getOr(j, "value", 0));
    if (type == "hasFlag")
        return std::make_unique<HasFlag>(ju::getOr<std::string>(j, "flag", ""));
    if (type == "affinityMin")
        return std::make_unique<AffinityMin>(ju::getOr<std::string>(j, "npc", ""),
                                             ju::getOr(j, "value", 0));
    if (type == "affinityAnyMin")
        return std::make_unique<AffinityAnyMin>(ju::getOr(j, "value", 0));
    if (type == "inventoryNotEmpty")
        return std::make_unique<InventoryNotEmpty>();
    if (type == "always")
        return std::make_unique<AlwaysTrue>();
    throw std::runtime_error("未知条件类型: \"" + type + "\"（events/endings.json 校验）");
}
````

## `src\events\Effect.cpp`

````cpp
#include "events/Effect.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <stdexcept>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "core/Random.h"
#include "economy/Market.h"
#include "events/Condition.h"
#include "events/EventScheduler.h"
#include "player/Inventory.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "story/NpcSystem.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

std::string MoneyEffect::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    const ConfigManager& cfg = ctx.config;
    if (amount_ >= 0) {
        ctx.player->wallet().add(amount_);
        return "现金 +" + std::to_string(amount_);
    }
    int want = -amount_;
    int take = std::min(ctx.player->wallet().get(), want);
    if (take > 0) ctx.player->wallet().spend(take);
    return take >= want ? "现金 -" + std::to_string(want)
                        : "现金 -" + std::to_string(take) + "（现金不足，掏空了钱包）";
}

std::string RandomMoneyEffect::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    int amount = ctx.rng.range(std::min(min_, max_), std::max(min_, max_));
    if (amount > 0) ctx.player->wallet().add(amount);
    return "现金 +" + std::to_string(amount);
}

std::string AffinityEffect::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    const NpcDef* n = ctx.config.npc(npcId_);
    if (!n) return "（无效的 NPC: " + npcId_ + "）";
    int before = ctx.npcs->affinity(npcId_);
    ctx.npcs->addAffinity(npcId_, delta_);
    int after = ctx.npcs->affinity(npcId_);
    std::string msg = n->name + " 好感 " + (delta_ >= 0 ? "+" : "") + std::to_string(delta_) +
                      "（" + std::to_string(before) + "→" + std::to_string(after) + "）";
    // 跨过解锁门槛时给出提示（分工 3.5 社交线回报）
    for (int gate : {30, 60, 80}) {
        if (before < gate && after >= gate) {
            msg += "｜解锁新渠道！";
            if (npcId_ == "roommate" && gate == 30) msg += "（室友每周多给一条二手货情报）";
            if (npcId_ == "senior" && gate == 30) msg += "（学长介绍打工，日薪 +20%）";
            if (npcId_ == "senior" && gate == 60) msg += "（考研资料进货价 9 折）";
            if (npcId_ == "xuejie" && gate == 30) msg += "（学姐带你进活动圈子）";
            if (npcId_ == "xuejie" && gate == 60) msg += "（演出内部票渠道开通）";
        }
    }
    return msg;
}

std::string FlagEffect::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    ctx.flags->set(flag_);
    return "获得剧情标记：" + flag_;
}

std::string PriceShockEffect::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    const GoodsDef* g = ctx.config.goods(goodsId_);
    if (!g) return "（无效的商品: " + goodsId_ + "）";
    ctx.market->applyShock(goodsId_, mult_, days_);
    return "「" + g->name + "」价格" + (mult_ >= 1.0 ? "暴涨" : "暴跌") + "（x" +
           std::to_string(mult_).substr(0, 4) + "，持续 " + std::to_string(days_) + " 天）";
}

std::string ItemEffect::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    const GoodsDef* g = ctx.config.goods(goodsId_);
    if (!g) return "（无效的商品: " + goodsId_ + "）";
    int fit = ctx.player->inventory().freeSlots() / g->slots;
    int realQty = std::min(qty_, fit);
    if (realQty <= 0) return "「" + g->name + "」x" + std::to_string(qty_) + " 放不下，只好放弃";
    ctx.player->inventory().add(goodsId_, realQty, 0.0, g->slots);
    std::string msg = "获得「" + g->name + "」x" + std::to_string(realQty);
    if (realQty < qty_) msg += "（背包满了，只装下一部分）";
    return msg;
}

int ItemEffect::estimateMoneyDelta() const {
    return 0; // 保守估计：免费物品不算现金收益
}

std::string StudyEffect::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    ctx.player->addStudy(delta_);
    if (delta_ > 0) ctx.time->markStudied();
    std::string sign = delta_ >= 0 ? "+" : "";
    return "平时分 " + sign + std::to_string(delta_) +
           "（当前 " + std::to_string(ctx.player->studyScore()) + "）";
}

std::string ScheduledEffect::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    int due = ctx.time->week() + weeks_;
    ctx.events->scheduleEffect(due, effectJson_);
    return "（这件事将在第 " + std::to_string(due) + " 周有结果）";
}

IfEffect::IfEffect(std::unique_ptr<ICondition> cond, std::vector<std::unique_ptr<IEffect>> effects)
    : cond_(std::move(cond)), effects_(std::move(effects)) {}

IfEffect::~IfEffect() = default;

std::string IfEffect::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    if (cond_ && !cond_->test(ctx)) return "";
    std::string out;
    for (const auto& e : effects_) {
        std::string m = e->apply(ctx);
        if (!m.empty()) out += (out.empty() ? "" : "；") + m;
    }
    return out;
}

int IfEffect::estimateMoneyDelta() const {
    int sum = 0;
    for (const auto& e : effects_) sum += e->estimateMoneyDelta();
    return sum;
}

std::unique_ptr<IEffect> EffectFactory::create(const Json& j) {
    std::string type = ju::getOr<std::string>(j, "type", "");
    if (type == "money")
        return std::make_unique<MoneyEffect>(ju::getOr(j, "amount", 0));
    if (type == "randomMoney")
        return std::make_unique<RandomMoneyEffect>(ju::getOr(j, "min", 0), ju::getOr(j, "max", 0));
    if (type == "affinity")
        return std::make_unique<AffinityEffect>(ju::getOr<std::string>(j, "npc", ""),
                                                ju::getOr(j, "delta", 0));
    if (type == "flag")
        return std::make_unique<FlagEffect>(ju::getOr<std::string>(j, "flag", ""));
    if (type == "priceShock")
        return std::make_unique<PriceShockEffect>(ju::getOr<std::string>(j, "goods", ""),
                                                  ju::getOr(j, "mult", 1.0),
                                                  ju::getOr(j, "days", 1));
    if (type == "item")
        return std::make_unique<ItemEffect>(ju::getOr<std::string>(j, "goods", ""),
                                            ju::getOr(j, "qty", 1));
    if (type == "study")
        return std::make_unique<StudyEffect>(ju::getOr(j, "delta", 0));
    if (type == "scheduled")
        return std::make_unique<ScheduledEffect>(ju::getOr(j, "weeks", 1), j.value("effect", Json::object()));
    if (type == "if") {
        auto cond = ConditionFactory::create(j.value("cond", Json::object()));
        std::vector<std::unique_ptr<IEffect>> effects;
        if (j.contains("effects") && j["effects"].is_array())
            for (const auto& e : j["effects"]) effects.push_back(EffectFactory::create(e));
        return std::make_unique<IfEffect>(std::move(cond), std::move(effects));
    }
    throw std::runtime_error("未知效果类型: \"" + type + "\"（events.json 校验）");
}
````

## `src\events\EndingJudge.cpp`

````cpp
#include "events/EndingJudge.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "events/Condition.h"

const EndingDef* EndingJudge::judge(const GameContext& ctx) {
    // 按 priority 排序判定（不修改 cfg 中的顺序）
    std::vector<const EndingDef*> sorted;
    for (const auto& e : ctx.config.endings()) sorted.push_back(&e);
    std::sort(sorted.begin(), sorted.end(),
              [](const EndingDef* a, const EndingDef* b) { return a->priority < b->priority; });

    for (const auto* e : sorted) {
        bool all = true;
        for (const auto& cj : e->conditions) {
            auto cond = ConditionFactory::create(cj);
            bool ok = cond->test(ctx);
#ifdef ENDING_DEBUG
            std::cout << "  [debug] ending=" << e->id << " type=" << cj.value("type", "?")
                      << " ok=" << ok << "\n";
#endif
            if (!ok) {
                all = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (all) return e;
    }
    throw std::runtime_error("endings.json 缺少兜底结局（priority=99, always）");
}
````

## `src\events\EventFactory.cpp`

````cpp
#include "events/EventFactory.h"

#include <set>
#include <stdexcept>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "events/Condition.h"
#include "events/Effect.h"
#include "events/GameEvent.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

std::unique_ptr<GameEvent> EventFactory::create(const Json& e, const ConfigManager& cfg) {
    std::string type = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "type", "random");
    std::string id = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "id", "");
    if (id.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("events.json 存在缺少 id 的事件");

    std::unique_ptr<GameEvent> ev;
    if (type == "random") {
        auto r = std::make_unique<RandomEvent>();
        r->weightValue = ju::getOr(e, "weight", 10);
        r->cooldownWeeks = ju::getOr(e, "cooldown", 2);
        ev = std::move(r);
    } else if (type == "fixed") {
        auto f = std::make_unique<FixedEvent>();
        f->week = ju::getOr(e, "week", 1);
        ev = std::move(f);
    } else if (type == "npc") {
        auto n = std::make_unique<NpcEvent>();
        n->npcId = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "npc", "");
        n->minAffinity = ju::getOr(e, "minAffinity", 0);
        if (!cfg.npc(n->npcId)) throw std::runtime_error("事件 " + id + " 引用了不存在的 NPC: " + n->npcId);
        ev = std::move(n);
    } else {
        throw std::runtime_error("事件 " + id + " 的类型未知: " + type);
    }

    ev->id = id;
    ev->name = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "name", id);
    ev->text = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "text", "");
    ev->once = ju::getOr(e, "once", false);

    if (e.contains("conditions") && e["conditions"].is_array())
        for (const auto& c : e["conditions"])
            ev->conditions.push_back(ConditionFactory::create(c));

    if (e.contains("choices") && e["choices"].is_array()) {
        for (const auto& c : e["choices"]) {
            GameEvent::Choice choice;
            choice.text = ju::getOr<std::string>(c, "text", "");
            if (c.contains("effects") && c["effects"].is_array())
                for (const auto& eff : c["effects"])
                    choice.effects.push_back(EffectFactory::create(eff));
            ev->choices.push_back(std::move(choice));
        }
    }
    if (ev->choices.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("事件 " + id + " 没有任何选项");
    return ev;
}

std::vector<std::unique_ptr<GameEvent>> EventFactory::buildAll(const ConfigManager& cfg) {
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<GameEvent>> out;
    std::set<std::string> ids;
    const Json& raw = cfg.rawEvents();
    for (const auto& e : raw) {
        auto ev = create(e, cfg);
        if (!ids.insert(ev->id).second)
            throw std::runtime_error("events.json 事件 id 重复: " + ev->id);
        out.push_back(std::move(ev));
    }
    return out;
}
````

## `src\events\EventScheduler.cpp`

````cpp
#include "events/EventScheduler.h"

#include <algorithm>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "core/Random.h"
#include "events/Effect.h"
#include "events/EventFactory.h"
#include "events/GameEvent.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

EventScheduler::EventScheduler(const ConfigManager& cfg, Random& rng) : cfg_(cfg), rng_(rng) {}

void EventScheduler::buildFromConfig() {
    all_ = EventFactory::buildAll(cfg_);
    cooldown_.clear();
    scheduled_.clear();
}

std::vector<const GameEvent*> EventScheduler::fixedForWeek(int week, const GameContext& ctx) const {
    std::vector<const GameEvent*> out;
    for (const auto& ev : all_) {
        auto* f = dynamic_cast<const FixedEvent*>(ev.get());
        if (f && f->week == week && ev->canTrigger(ctx)) out.push_back(ev.get());
    }
    return out;
}

const GameEvent* EventScheduler::rollRandom(const GameContext& ctx) {
    // 候选：随机事件 + 条件通过 + 冷却结束
    std::vector<const RandomEvent*> candidates;
    std::vector<int> weights;
    for (const auto& ev : all_) {
        auto* r = dynamic_cast<const RandomEvent*>(ev.get());
        if (!r) continue;
        auto it = cooldown_.find(r->id);
        if (it != cooldown_.end() && it->second > 0) continue;
        if (!r->canTrigger(ctx)) continue;
        candidates.push_back(r);
        weights.push_back(std::max(1, r->weight(ctx)));
    }
    if (candidates.empty()) return nullptr;
    int total = 0;
    for (int w : weights) total += w;
    int roll = rng_.range(1, total);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < candidates.size(); ++i) {
        roll -= weights[i];
        if (roll <= 0) return candidates[i];
    }
    return candidates.back();
}

void EventScheduler::markTriggered(const GameEvent& ev) {
    auto* r = dynamic_cast<const RandomEvent*>(&ev);
    if (r && r->cooldownWeeks > 0) cooldown_[r->id] = r->cooldownWeeks + 1; // 本周结算时递减一次
}

void EventScheduler::scheduleEffect(int dueWeek, Json effectJson) {
    scheduled_.emplace_back(dueWeek, std::move(effectJson));
}

void EventScheduler::applyDueEffects(GameContext& ctx) {
    // 周结算发生在周翻转之后：正在结算的是 time->settledWeek()
    int now = ctx.time->settledWeek();
    for (auto it = scheduled_.begin(); it != scheduled_.end();) {
        if (it->first <= now) {
            auto eff = EffectFactory::create(it->second);
            eff->apply(ctx);
            it = scheduled_.erase(it);
        } else {
            ++it;
        }
    }
}

void EventScheduler::onWeekStart() {
    for (auto it = cooldown_.begin(); it != cooldown_.end();) {
        if (--it->second <= 0) it = cooldown_.erase(it);
        else ++it;
    }
}

Json EventScheduler::toJson() const {
    Json cd = Json::object();
    for (const auto& [id, w] : cooldown_) cd[id] = w;
    Json sched = Json::array();
    for (const auto& [week, eff] : scheduled_)
        sched.push_back(Json{{"week", week}, {"effect", eff}});
    return Json{{"cooldown", cd}, {"scheduled", sched}};
}

void EventScheduler::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    cooldown_.clear();
    if (j.contains("cooldown") && j["cooldown"].is_object())
        for (auto it = j["cooldown"].begin(); it != j["cooldown"].end(); ++it)
            cooldown_[it.key()] = it.value().get<int>();
    scheduled_.clear();
    if (j.contains("scheduled") && j["scheduled"].is_array())
        for (const auto& e : j["scheduled"])
            scheduled_.emplace_back(ju::getOr(e, "week", 1), e.value("effect", Json::object()));
}
````

## `src\events\GameEvent.cpp`

````cpp
#include "events/GameEvent.h"

#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "core/IEffect.h"
#include "events/Condition.h"
#include "story/NpcSystem.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"

bool GameEvent::canTrigger(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    if (once && ctx.flags->has("evt_done_" + id)) return false;
    for (const auto& c : conditions)
        if (!c->test(ctx)) return false;
    return true;
}

void GameEvent::apply(GameContext& ctx) const {
    if (choices.empty()) return;
    for (const auto& e : choices.front().effects) e->apply(ctx);
    ctx.flags->set("evt_done_" + id);
}

bool FixedEvent::canTrigger(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    if (ctx.time->week() != week) return false;
    return GameEvent::canTrigger(ctx);
}

bool NpcEvent::canTrigger(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    if (!ctx.npcs->atLeast(npcId, minAffinity)) return false;
    return GameEvent::canTrigger(ctx);
}
````

## `src\player\Energy.cpp`

````cpp
#include "player/Energy.h"

#include <algorithm>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

EnergySystem::EnergySystem(const ConfigManager& cfg)
    : max_(cfg.balance().staminaMax),
      lowThreshold_(cfg.balance().staminaLowThreshold),
      stamina_(cfg.balance().staminaMax) {}

bool EnergySystem::isLow() const {
    return stamina_ < lowThreshold_;
}

bool EnergySystem::spend(int cost) {
    if (cost < 0 || stamina_ < cost) return false;
    stamina_ -= cost;
    return true;
}

void EnergySystem::add(int amount) {
    if (amount <= 0) return;
    stamina_ = std::min(max_, stamina_ + amount);
}

Json EnergySystem::toJson() const {
    return Json{{"stamina", stamina_}, {"max", max_}};
}

void EnergySystem::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    stamina_ = ju::getOr(j, "stamina", stamina_);
    stamina_ = std::clamp(stamina_, 0, max_);
}
````

## `src\player\GoalTracker.cpp`

````cpp
#include "player/GoalTracker.h"

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "player/Energy.h"
#include "player/Inventory.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "player/TradeSystem.h"

GoalSnapshot GoalTracker::snapshot(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    GoalSnapshot s;
    const BalanceDef& b = ctx.config.balance();

    s.cash = ctx.player->wallet().get();
    s.inventoryValue = ctx.trade->estimatePortfolioValue(ctx);  // 别重写公式
    s.totalAssets = s.cash + s.inventoryValue;
    s.targetMoney = b.targetMoney;

    s.studyScore = ctx.player->studyScore();
    s.passScore = b.passScore;
    s.failed = ctx.player->study().failed();
    s.maxAffinity = ctx.npcs->maxAffinity();

    s.week = ctx.time->week();
    s.weeks = b.weeks;
    s.day = ctx.time->day();
    s.hour = ctx.time->hour();

    s.stamina = ctx.player->energy().get();
    s.staminaMax = ctx.player->energy().max();
    s.slotsUsed = ctx.player->inventory().usedSlots();
    s.slotsMax = ctx.player->inventory().capacity();
    return s;
}
````

## `src\player\Inventory.cpp`

````cpp
#include "player/Inventory.h"

#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

int Inventory::usedSlots() const {
    int s = 0;
    for (const auto& it : items_) s += it.qty * it.slotsPer;
    return s;
}

const Inventory::Item* Inventory::find(const std::string& goodsId) const {
    for (const auto& it : items_)
        if (it.goodsId == goodsId) return &it;
    return nullptr;
}

Inventory::Item* Inventory::findMutable(const std::string& goodsId) {
    for (auto& it : items_)
        if (it.goodsId == goodsId) return &it;
    return nullptr;
}

void Inventory::add(const std::string& goodsId, int qty, double unitCost, int slotsPer) {
    if (qty <= 0) return;
    Item* it = findMutable(goodsId);
    if (it) {
        double totalCost = it->avgCost * it->qty + unitCost * qty;
        it->qty += qty;
        it->avgCost = totalCost / it->qty;
    } else {
        items_.push_back({goodsId, qty, slotsPer, unitCost});
    }
}

bool Inventory::remove(const std::string& goodsId, int qty) {
    Item* it = findMutable(goodsId);
    if (!it || it->qty < qty) return false;
    it->qty -= qty;
    if (it->qty == 0) {
        // 保持顺序，移除空堆叠
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < items_.size(); ++i) {
            if (items_[i].goodsId == goodsId) {
                items_.erase(items_.begin() + static_cast<long>(i));
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

Json Inventory::toJson() const {
    Json arr = Json::array();
    for (const auto& it : items_) {
        arr.push_back(Json{
            {"goodsId", it.goodsId},
            {"qty", it.qty},
            {"slotsPer", it.slotsPer},
            {"avgCost", it.avgCost},
        });
    }
    return Json{{"capacity", capacity_}, {"items", arr}};
}

void Inventory::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    capacity_ = ju::getOr(j, "capacity", capacity_);
    items_.clear();
    if (!j.contains("items") || !j["items"].is_array()) return;
    for (const auto& e : j["items"]) {
        Item it;
        it.goodsId  = ju::getOr<std::string>(e, "goodsId", "");
        it.qty      = ju::getOr(e, "qty", 0);
        it.slotsPer = ju::getOr(e, "slotsPer", 1);
        it.avgCost  = ju::getOr(e, "avgCost", 0.0);
        if (!it.goodsId.empty() && it.qty > 0) items_.push_back(std::move(it));
    }
}
````

## `src\player\JobSystem.cpp`

````cpp
#include "player/JobSystem.h"

#include <algorithm>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "core/Random.h"
#include "player/Energy.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "story/NpcSystem.h"

JobResult JobSystem::work(GameContext& ctx) const {
    const BalanceDef& b = ctx.config.balance();

    auto jobIt = b.jobs.find(ctx.locationId);
    const int lo = (jobIt == b.jobs.end()) ? 80  : jobIt->second.first;
    const int hi = (jobIt == b.jobs.end()) ? 100 : jobIt->second.second;

    JobResult r;
    auto nameIt = b.jobNames.find(ctx.locationId);
    r.jobName = (nameIt != b.jobNames.end()) ? nameIt->second : "打零工";

    r.pay = ctx.rng.range(lo, hi);
    if (ctx.npcs->atLeast("senior", 30))
        r.pay = static_cast<int>(r.pay * b.seniorWorkBonus);

    r.lowEnergy = ctx.player->energy().isLow();          // < 30
    if (r.lowEnergy) r.pay = static_cast<int>(r.pay * b.lowEnergyPayRate);

    auto stIt = b.jobStamina.find(ctx.locationId);
    const int cost = (stIt != b.jobStamina.end()) ? stIt->second : 20;
    r.staminaCost = std::min(cost, ctx.player->energy().get()); // 扣到 0 为止，不为负
    ctx.player->energy().spend(r.staminaCost);
    ctx.player->wallet().add(r.pay);

    r.msg = r.jobName + "干了一天，工资 +¥" + std::to_string(r.pay);
    if (r.lowEnergy) r.msg += "（体力不支，只干出半份活）";
    return r;
}
````

````cpp
#include "player/Player.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

Player::Player(const ConfigManager& cfg)
    : inventory_(cfg.balance().maxSlots),
      energy_(cfg),
      study_(cfg),
      stockSkill_(cfg.balance().stockSkillStart),
      stockSkillMax_(cfg.balance().stockSkillMax) {}

void Player::onTradeCompleted(const TradeCompletedEvent& e) {
    const GoodsDef* g = nullptr; // 文本由调用方补充商品名太绕，这里只记 id/qty/金额
    (void)g;
    char buf[128];
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%s %s x%d 金额 %d",
                  e.isBuy ? "买入" : "卖出", e.goodsId.c_str(), e.qty, e.total);
    ledger_.push_back(buf);
    if (ledger_.size() > 200) ledger_.erase(ledger_.begin()); // 流水上限，防膨胀
}

Json Player::toJson() const {
    return Json{
        {"wallet", wallet_.toJson()},
        {"inventory", inventory_.toJson()},
        {"studyScore", study_.score()},   // 旧版本仍能读
        {"study", study_.toJson()},       // 新字段：failed
        {"stamina", energy_.toJson()},
        {"stockSkill", stockSkill_},
        {"ledger", ledger_},
    };
}

void Player::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    if (j.contains("wallet")) wallet_.fromJson(j["wallet"]);
    if (j.contains("inventory")) inventory_.fromJson(j["inventory"]);
    if (j.contains("study")) study_.fromJson(j["study"]);
    else study_.setScore(ju::getOr(j, "studyScore", study_.score()));  // 旧档
    stockSkill_ = ju::getOr(j, "stockSkill", stockSkill_);
    if (stockSkill_ < 0) stockSkill_ = 0;
    if (stockSkill_ > stockSkillMax_) stockSkill_ = stockSkillMax_;
    if (j.contains("stamina")) energy_.fromJson(j["stamina"]);
    ledger_.clear();
    if (j.contains("ledger") && j["ledger"].is_array())
        for (const auto& e : j["ledger"]) ledger_.push_back(e.get<std::string>());
    if (ledger_.size() > 200) ledger_.erase(ledger_.begin(), ledger_.begin() + static_cast<long>(ledger_.size() - 200));
}
````

## `src\player\StudySystem.cpp`

````cpp
#include "player/StudySystem.h"

#include <algorithm>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "player/Energy.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

StudySystem::StudySystem(const ConfigManager& cfg)
    : cfg_(cfg), score_(cfg.balance().studyStart) {}

void StudySystem::setScore(int v) { score_ = std::clamp(v, 0, 100); }

int StudySystem::study(StudyMode mode, EnergySystem& energy, int week, std::string& msg) {
    const BalanceDef& b = cfg_.balance();
    bool night = (mode == StudyMode::AllNighter);

    // 熬夜要体力；不够就退化成普通自习（文案告诉玩家）
    const bool fellBack = night && !energy.spend(b.studyNightStamina);
    if (fellBack) night = false;

    // msg 每次都要重写：它是出参，调用方的 std::string 会复用
    msg = fellBack ? "太累了，熬夜看不进去，改成普通自习。"
                   : (night ? "在图书馆刷夜" : "泡了一天图书馆");

    int gain = b.studyGain + (night ? b.studyNightBonus : 0);
    if (week >= b.examStartWeek) gain += b.examStudyBonus;   // 考试周冲刺

    const int before = score_;
    score_ = std::min(100, score_ + gain);
    return score_ - before;
}

StudyWeekOutcome StudySystem::onWeekEnd(int week, bool studiedThisWeek) {
    StudyWeekOutcome o;
    const BalanceDef& b = cfg_.balance();

    if (week > 1 && !studiedThisWeek) {
        o.decay = std::min(b.studyDecay, score_);   // 不会扣成负数
        score_ -= o.decay;
    }
    if (week == b.midtermWeek && score_ < b.warnScore) o.midtermWarn = true;
    if (week == b.weeks) {
        if (score_ < b.passScore) {
            failed_ = true;
            o.failedNow = true;
            o.retakeFee = b.retakeFee;
        } else if (score_ >= b.scholarshipScore) {
            o.scholarship = b.scholarship;
        }
    }
    return o;
}

Json StudySystem::toJson() const {
    return Json{{"score", score_}, {"failed", failed_}};
}

void StudySystem::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    score_ = std::clamp(ju::getOr(j, "score", score_), 0, 100);
    failed_ = ju::getOr(j, "failed", failed_);
}
````

## `src\player\TradeSystem.cpp`

````cpp
#include "player/TradeSystem.h"

#include <algorithm>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/DomainEvents.h"
#include "core/EventBus.h"
#include "economy/Market.h"
#include "player/Inventory.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"

void TradeSystem::passMarketHour(GameContext& ctx) const {
    ctx.time->spendHours(ctx.config.balance().tradeHours);
}

TradeResult TradeSystem::fail(TradeError c, const std::string& m,
                              int maxQty, int unitPrice) const {
    TradeResult r;
    r.ok = false;
    r.msg = m;
    r.code = c;
    r.maxQty = maxQty;
    r.unitPrice = unitPrice;
    return r;
}

TradeResult TradeSystem::buy(GameContext& ctx, const std::string& goodsId, int qty) const {
    const GoodsDef* g = cfg_.goods(goodsId);
    if (!g) return fail(TradeError::UnknownGoods, "没有这种商品: " + goodsId);
    if (qty <= 0) return fail(TradeError::InvalidCount, "数量必须为正");

    // 1) 地点：这里有没有卖
    const LocationDef* loc = cfg_.location(ctx.locationId);
    const bool soldHere =
        std::find(g->soldAt.begin(), g->soldAt.end(), ctx.locationId) != g->soldAt.end();
    if (!soldHere)
        return fail(TradeError::WrongLocation,
                    "「" + g->name + "」在" + (loc ? loc->name : ctx.locationId) + "没有卖");

    // 2) 报价（社交线折扣保留）
    int unit = ctx.market->buyPrice(ctx.locationId, goodsId);
    if (goodsId == "kaoyan_notes" && ctx.flags->has("senior_notes"))
        unit = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(unit * cfg_.balance().kaoyanDiscount));

    // 3) 三个上限：限购 / 容量 / 现金
    const int quota   = ctx.market->quotaLeft(ctx.locationId, goodsId);
    const int fit     = ctx.player->inventory().freeSlots() / g->slots;
    const int afford  = unit > 0 ? ctx.player->wallet().get() / unit : 0;
    const int maxQty  = std::min({quota, fit, afford});

    // 4) 一件都买不了：说清是哪条卡住
    if (maxQty <= 0) {
        if (quota <= 0)
            return fail(TradeError::QuotaExceeded,
                        "「" + g->name + "」今天在这里的限购额度已用完，明天再来", 0, unit);
        if (fit <= 0)
            return fail(TradeError::NotEnoughSlot, "背包塞不下了，先卖点东西吧", 0, unit);
        return fail(TradeError::NotEnoughCash, "现金不够，先打工或者卖货吧", 0, unit);
    }

    // 5) 买不到请求的数量：拒绝（不再静默减量）
    if (qty > maxQty) {
        const bool byQuota = (quota == maxQty);
        const bool bySlot  = (!byQuota && fit == maxQty);
        const TradeError code = byQuota ? TradeError::QuotaExceeded
                              : bySlot  ? TradeError::NotEnoughSlot
                                        : TradeError::NotEnoughCash;
        const char* why = byQuota ? "限购只剩 " : bySlot ? "背包只放得下 " : "现金只够买 ";
        return fail(code, std::string("最多可买 ") + std::to_string(maxQty) + " 件（" +
                          why + std::to_string(maxQty) + "）", maxQty, unit);
    }

    // 6) 成交（跑一趟市场：1 小时）
    const int total = unit * qty;
    ctx.player->wallet().spend(total);
    ctx.player->inventory().add(goodsId, qty, static_cast<double>(unit), g->slots);
    ctx.market->consumeQuota(ctx.locationId, goodsId, qty);
    passMarketHour(ctx);
    ctx.bus.publish(TradeCompletedEvent{goodsId, qty, total, true});

    TradeResult r;
    r.ok = true;
    r.msg = "买入「" + g->name + "」x" + std::to_string(qty) +
            "，支出 ¥" + std::to_string(total);
    r.amount = total;
    r.unitPrice = unit;
    r.maxQty = qty;          // 成功时也填，UI 可以统一显示
    return r;
}

TradeResult TradeSystem::sell(GameContext& ctx, const std::string& goodsId, int qty) const {
    const GoodsDef* g = cfg_.goods(goodsId);
    if (!g) return fail(TradeError::UnknownGoods, "没有这种商品: " + goodsId);
    if (qty <= 0) return fail(TradeError::InvalidCount, "数量必须为正");

    const Inventory::Item* item = ctx.player->inventory().find(goodsId);
    if (!item)
        return fail(TradeError::NotOwned, "背包里没有「" + g->name + "」");

    int unit = ctx.market->sellPrice(ctx.locationId, goodsId);
    // 社交线加成：学姐内部票（演出门票卖出 +25%）
    if (goodsId == "show_ticket" && ctx.flags->has("inner_ticket"))
        unit = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(unit * cfg_.balance().innerTicketBonus));

    if (qty > item->qty)
        return fail(TradeError::NotOwned,
                    "你只有 " + std::to_string(item->qty) + " 件「" + g->name + "」",
                    item->qty, unit);

    const int total = unit * qty;
    const double costBasis = item->avgCost * qty;   // 必须在 remove 之前取
    ctx.player->wallet().add(total);
    ctx.player->inventory().remove(goodsId, qty);
    passMarketHour(ctx); // 成交一趟市场：1 小时
    ctx.bus.publish(TradeCompletedEvent{goodsId, qty, total, false});

    const long long profit = static_cast<long long>(total) - static_cast<long long>(costBasis);
    TradeResult r;
    r.ok = true;
    r.msg = "卖出「" + g->name + "」x" + std::to_string(qty) +
            "，收入 ¥" + std::to_string(total);
    if (profit >= 0) r.msg += "（盈利 ¥" + std::to_string(profit) + "）";
    else             r.msg += "（亏损 ¥" + std::to_string(-profit) + "）";
    r.amount = total;
    r.unitPrice = unit;
    r.maxQty = qty;
    return r;
}

int TradeSystem::estimatePortfolioValue(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    int value = 0;
    for (const auto& item : ctx.player->inventory().items()) {
        int best = 0;
        for (const auto& loc : cfg_.locations()) {
            int p = ctx.market->sellPrice(loc.id, item.goodsId);
            if (p > best) best = p;
        }
        value += best * item.qty;
    }
    return value;
}
````
