## `src\save\SaveManager.cpp`

````cpp
#include "save/SaveManager.h"

#include <cstdio>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <stdexcept>

#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

namespace fs = std::filesystem;

std::string SaveManager::pathFor(int slot) const {
    return dir_ + "/slot" + std::to_string(slot) + ".json";
}

void SaveManager::ensureDir() const {
    std::error_code ec;
    fs::create_directories(dir_, ec); // 已存在也不报错
}

bool SaveManager::save(int slot, const Json& data, std::string* err) {
    ensureDir();
    const std::string path = pathFor(slot);
    const std::string tmp = path + ".tmp";
    try {
        ju::saveFile(tmp, data);
        // 原子替换：先删旧档再改名（窗口极小；坏档有版本号校验兜底）
        std::error_code ec;
        fs::remove(path, ec);
        fs::rename(tmp, path, ec);
        if (ec) throw std::runtime_error("重命名存档失败: " + ec.message());
        return true;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        if (err) *err = e.what();
        return false;
    }
}

Json SaveManager::load(int slot, std::string* err) {
    const std::string path = pathFor(slot);
    try {
        Json j = ju::loadFile(path);
        if (!j.is_object()) throw std::runtime_error("存档格式不是对象");
        int version = ju::getOr(j, "version", 0);
        if (version <= 0) throw std::runtime_error("存档缺少版本号（可能是坏档）");
        if (version > 1) throw std::runtime_error("存档版本过新（" + std::to_string(version) + "），无法读取");
        return j;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        if (err) *err = e.what();
        return Json{};
    }
}

std::vector<SaveManager::SlotInfo> SaveManager::listSlots() const {
    std::vector<SlotInfo> out;
    for (int slot = 0; slot <= kManualSlots; ++slot) {
        SlotInfo info;
        info.slot = slot;
        try {
            Json j = ju::loadFile(pathFor(slot));
            info.exists = true;
            info.version = ju::getOr(j, "version", 0);
            if (j.contains("meta")) {
                int week = ju::getOr(j["meta"], "week", 0);
                int money = ju::getOr(j["meta"], "money", 0);
                std::string loc = ju::getOr<std::string>(j["meta"], "location", "?");
                info.summary = "第" + std::to_string(week) + "周 · ¥" + std::to_string(money) + " · " + loc;
            }
        } catch (...) {
            info.exists = false;
        }
        out.push_back(info);
    }
    return out;
}
````

## `src\simulate\BotStrategy.cpp`

````cpp
#include "simulate/BotStrategy.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>

#include "actions/Commands.h"
#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "core/IEffect.h"
#include "core/Random.h"
#include "core/Session.h"
#include "core/WeekSettlement.h"
#include "economy/Market.h"
#include "events/GameEvent.h"
#include "player/Inventory.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "player/TradeSystem.h"
#include "story/NpcSystem.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"

namespace {

// 简单解析器：选"估算现金收益最大"的选项（IEffect::estimateMoneyDelta 多态求和）
class SimResolver : public IChoiceResolver {
public:
    int chooseChoice(const GameEvent& ev, const GameContext& ctx) const override {
        (void)ctx;
        int best = 0;
        long long bestValue = std::numeric_limits<long long>::min();
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < ev.choices.size(); ++i) {
            long long v = 0;
            for (const auto& e : ev.choices[i].effects) v += e->estimateMoneyDelta();
            if (v > bestValue) {
                bestValue = v;
                best = static_cast<int>(i);
            }
        }
        return best;
    }
};

// 执行一条消耗时间的命令；途中触发的随机事件由 resolver 立即解决
bool runApCommand(GameContext& ctx, const ICommand& cmd, const IChoiceResolver& resolver) {
    ActionOutcome out = Session::runAction(ctx, cmd);
    if (out.event) Session::applyChoice(ctx, *out.event, resolver.chooseChoice(*out.event, ctx));
    return out.result.ok && out.result.consumedTime;
}

// Bot 社交（v2 选项池版）：从正分池抽一条话题，稳定 +5 好感
bool botSocialize(GameContext& ctx) {
    const LocationDef* loc = ctx.config.location(ctx.locationId);
    if (!loc || loc->npcId.empty()) return false;
    const SocialPoolDef* pool = ctx.config.socialPool(loc->npcId);
    if (!pool || pool->positive.empty()) return false;
    const std::string& text = pool->positive[ctx.rng.next() % pool->positive.size()];
    ActionOutcome out = Session::runAction(ctx, SocialPickCommand(loc->npcId, text, +5));
    if (out.event) Session::applyChoice(ctx, *out.event, defaultResolver().chooseChoice(*out.event, ctx));
    return out.result.ok && out.result.consumedTime;
}

// 深夜规则：23 点后必须回宿舍睡觉（避免熬过凌晨 2 点昏迷）
// 注意：周结算由模拟循环统一在周翻转后触发，这里不重复结算
bool nightSleep(GameContext& ctx, const IChoiceResolver& resolver) {
    if (ctx.locationId != "dorm") return runApCommand(ctx, MoveCommand("dorm"), resolver);
    return runApCommand(ctx, SleepCommand(), resolver);
}

// 某商品在各地的最高卖价（及地点 id）
std::pair<int, std::string> bestSell(GameContext& ctx, const std::string& goodsId) {
    int best = 0;
    std::string bestLoc;
    for (const auto& l : ctx.config.locations()) {
        int p = ctx.market->sellPrice(l.id, goodsId);
        if (p > best) {
            best = p;
            bestLoc = l.id;
        }
    }
    return {best, bestLoc};
}

// ---------------- 只倒卖：低买高卖，周内周转 ----------------
class TraderBot : public BotStrategy {
public:
    std::string name() const override { return "trader"; }
    bool takeTurn(GameContext& ctx) override {
        if (!ctx.time->canAct()) return false;
        if (ctx.time->hour() >= 23) return nightSleep(ctx, resolver_);

        // 学业底线：期中前把分数拉到 58 以上
        if (ctx.time->week() >= 5 && ctx.player->studyScore() < 58) {
            if (ctx.locationId != "library") return runApCommand(ctx, MoveCommand("library"), resolver_);
            return runApCommand(ctx, StudyCommand(), resolver_);
        }
        // 1) 有库存 → 去最优地点卖
        const auto& items = ctx.player->inventory().items();
        if (!items.empty()) {
            const Inventory::Item* bestItem = nullptr;
            int bestProfit = -1;
            std::string bestLoc;
            for (const auto& it : items) {
                auto [p, loc] = bestSell(ctx, it.goodsId);
                int profit = (p - static_cast<int>(it.avgCost)) * it.qty;
                if (profit > bestProfit) {
                    bestProfit = profit;
                    bestItem = &it;
                    bestLoc = loc;
                }
            }
            if (bestItem && bestProfit > 0) {
                if (ctx.locationId == bestLoc) {
                    // 就地清仓所有"此地即最优"的货（免行动点）
                    for (const auto& it : items) {
                        if (bestSell(ctx, it.goodsId).second != ctx.locationId) continue;
                        const Inventory::Item* cur = ctx.player->inventory().find(it.goodsId);
                        if (cur) ctx.trade->sell(ctx, it.goodsId, cur->qty);
                    }
                    return false;
                }
                return runApCommand(ctx, MoveCommand(bestLoc), resolver_);
            }
        }

        // 2) 扫描进货机会：毛利/占格 最大者
        int bestMargin = -1;
        std::string bestGoods, bestBuyLoc;
        for (const auto& g : ctx.config.goods()) {
            if (!ctx.player->inventory().canFit(1, g.slots)) continue;
            auto [sellP, sellLoc] = bestSell(ctx, g.id);
            for (const auto& buyLocId : g.soldAt) {
                int buyP = ctx.market->buyPrice(buyLocId, g.id);
                if (ctx.market->quotaLeft(buyLocId, g.id) <= 0) continue;
                int margin = sellP - buyP;
                if (margin <= 0) continue;
                int marginPerSlot = margin / g.slots;
                if (marginPerSlot > bestMargin) {
                    bestMargin = marginPerSlot;
                    bestGoods = g.id;
                    bestBuyLoc = buyLocId;
                }
            }
        }
        const GoodsDef* bg = bestGoods.empty() ? nullptr : ctx.config.goods(bestGoods);
        if (bg) {
            int buyP = ctx.market->buyPrice(bestBuyLoc, bestGoods);
            auto [sellP, sellLoc] = bestSell(ctx, bestGoods);
            double ratio = buyP > 0 ? static_cast<double>(sellP - buyP) / buyP : 0.0;
            if (ratio > 0.12 && sellLoc != bestBuyLoc) { // 12% 毛利才出手，防止无意义倒手
                if (ctx.locationId == bestBuyLoc) {
                    int affordable = buyP > 0 ? ctx.player->wallet().get() / buyP : 0;
                    int fit = ctx.player->inventory().freeSlots() / bg->slots;
                    int qty = std::min({ctx.market->quotaLeft(bestBuyLoc, bestGoods), affordable, fit});
                    if (qty > 0) {
                        ctx.trade->buy(ctx, bestGoods, qty);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                return runApCommand(ctx, MoveCommand(bestBuyLoc), resolver_);
            }
        }

        // 3) 兜底：打工
        return runApCommand(ctx, WorkCommand(), resolver_);
    }

private:
    SimResolver resolver_;
};

// ---------------- 只打工：稳定但天花板低（验证"单一策略不优"） ----------------
class WorkerBot : public BotStrategy {
public:
    std::string name() const override { return "worker"; }
    bool takeTurn(GameContext& ctx) override {
        if (!ctx.time->canAct()) return false;
        if (ctx.time->hour() >= 23) return nightSleep(ctx, resolver_);
        // 学业及格线策略（保住 60 分即可，不为奖学金浪费行动点）
        if (ctx.player->studyScore() < 62 ||
            (ctx.time->week() >= 6 && !ctx.time->studiedThisWeek() && ctx.player->studyScore() < 70)) {
            if (ctx.locationId != "library") return runApCommand(ctx, MoveCommand("library"), resolver_);
            return runApCommand(ctx, StudyCommand(), resolver_);
        }
        // 其余时间打工
        return runApCommand(ctx, WorkCommand(), resolver_);
    }

private:
    SimResolver resolver_;
};

// ---------------- 混合：打工攒本 + 高毛利倒卖 + 兼顾学业 ----------------
class MixedBot : public BotStrategy {
public:
    std::string name() const override { return "mixed"; }
    bool takeTurn(GameContext& ctx) override {
        if (!ctx.time->canAct()) return false;
        if (ctx.time->hour() >= 23) return nightSleep(ctx, resolver_);

        // 学业：稳在及格线上方（不冲奖学金——行动点拿去经营更划算）
        if (ctx.player->studyScore() < 70 ||
            (ctx.time->week() >= 6 && !ctx.time->studiedThisWeek() && ctx.player->studyScore() < 74)) {
            if (ctx.locationId != "library") return runApCommand(ctx, MoveCommand("library"), resolver_);
            return runApCommand(ctx, StudyCommand(), resolver_);
        }

        // 清仓（同 TraderBot）
        const auto& items = ctx.player->inventory().items();
        if (!items.empty()) {
            std::string bestLoc;
            int bestProfit = -1;
            for (const auto& it : items) {
                auto [p, loc] = bestSell(ctx, it.goodsId);
                int profit = (p - static_cast<int>(it.avgCost)) * it.qty;
                if (profit > bestProfit) {
                    bestProfit = profit;
                    bestLoc = loc;
                }
            }
            if (bestProfit > 0) {
                if (ctx.locationId == bestLoc) {
                    for (const auto& it : items) {
                        if (bestSell(ctx, it.goodsId).second != ctx.locationId) continue;
                        const Inventory::Item* cur = ctx.player->inventory().find(it.goodsId);
                        if (cur) ctx.trade->sell(ctx, it.goodsId, cur->qty);
                    }
                    return false;
                }
                return runApCommand(ctx, MoveCommand(bestLoc), resolver_);
            }
        }

        // 社交底线：好结局需要一位好友（好感 ≥60），空闲时维护到 80
        bool urgentSocial = ctx.npcs->maxAffinity() < 60;
        bool casualSocial = ctx.npcs->maxAffinity() < 80;
        if (urgentSocial || casualSocial) {
            const LocationDef* loc = ctx.config.location(ctx.locationId);
            bool npcHere = loc && !loc->npcId.empty();
            if (npcHere && (urgentSocial || !hasGoodBuyOpportunity(ctx)))
                return botSocialize(ctx);
            if (urgentSocial) return runApCommand(ctx, MoveCommand("dorm"), resolver_);
        }

        // 本金少先打工攒本
        if (ctx.player->wallet().get() < 1200) return runApCommand(ctx, WorkCommand(), resolver_);

        // 高毛利才进货（13% 门槛，比纯倒卖略保守）
        int bestMargin = -1;
        std::string bestGoods, bestBuyLoc;
        for (const auto& g : ctx.config.goods()) {
            if (!ctx.player->inventory().canFit(1, g.slots)) continue;
            auto [sellP, sellLoc] = bestSell(ctx, g.id);
            if (sellLoc.empty() || sellLoc == "?") continue;
            for (const auto& buyLocId : g.soldAt) {
                if (buyLocId == sellLoc) continue; // 混合策略不原地倒手
                int buyP = ctx.market->buyPrice(buyLocId, g.id);
                if (ctx.market->quotaLeft(buyLocId, g.id) <= 0) continue;
                int marginPerSlot = (sellP - buyP) / g.slots;
                if (marginPerSlot > bestMargin) {
                    bestMargin = marginPerSlot;
                    bestGoods = g.id;
                    bestBuyLoc = buyLocId;
                }
            }
        }
        const GoodsDef* bg = bestGoods.empty() ? nullptr : ctx.config.goods(bestGoods);
        if (bg) {
            int buyP = ctx.market->buyPrice(bestBuyLoc, bestGoods);
            auto [sellP, sellLoc] = bestSell(ctx, bestGoods);
            double ratio = buyP > 0 ? static_cast<double>(sellP - buyP) / buyP : 0.0;
            if (ratio > 0.13) {
                if (ctx.locationId == bestBuyLoc) {
                    int affordable = buyP > 0 ? ctx.player->wallet().get() / buyP : 0;
                    int fit = ctx.player->inventory().freeSlots() / bg->slots;
                    int qty = std::min({ctx.market->quotaLeft(bestBuyLoc, bestGoods), affordable, fit});
                    if (qty > 0) {
                        ctx.trade->buy(ctx, bestGoods, qty);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                return runApCommand(ctx, MoveCommand(bestBuyLoc), resolver_);
            }
        }
        return runApCommand(ctx, WorkCommand(), resolver_);
    }

private:
    SimResolver resolver_;

    // 是否存在值得出手的进货机会（毛利 ≥13%）
    bool hasGoodBuyOpportunity(GameContext& ctx) const {
        for (const auto& g : ctx.config.goods()) {
            if (!ctx.player->inventory().canFit(1, g.slots)) continue;
            auto [sellP, sellLoc] = bestSell(ctx, g.id);
            if (sellLoc.empty()) continue;
            for (const auto& buyLocId : g.soldAt) {
                if (buyLocId == sellLoc) continue;
                int buyP = ctx.market->buyPrice(buyLocId, g.id);
                if (buyP > 0 && ctx.market->quotaLeft(buyLocId, g.id) > 0 &&
                    static_cast<double>(sellP - buyP) / buyP > 0.13)
                    return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
};

} // namespace

const IChoiceResolver& defaultResolver() {
    static SimResolver r;
    return r;
}

std::unique_ptr<BotStrategy> makeStrategy(const std::string& name) {
    if (name == "trader") return std::make_unique<TraderBot>();
    if (name == "worker") return std::make_unique<WorkerBot>();
    if (name == "mixed") return std::make_unique<MixedBot>();
    throw std::runtime_error("未知策略: " + name + "（可选 trader/worker/mixed）");
}
````

## `src\simulate\SimulationRunner.cpp`

````cpp
#include "simulate/SimulationRunner.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <map>
#include <stdexcept>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/EventBus.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "core/Random.h"
#include "core/Session.h"
#include "core/WeekSettlement.h"
#include "events/EndingJudge.h"
#include "events/EventScheduler.h"
#include "events/GameEvent.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "player/TradeSystem.h"
#include "simulate/BotStrategy.h"
#include "story/NpcSystem.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"

SimGameResult SimulationRunner::runOne(const std::string& strategyName, std::uint64_t seed) {
    Random rng(seed);
    EventBus bus;
    GameContext ctx(cfg_, rng, bus);
    ctx.initNewGame();

    auto bot = makeStrategy(strategyName);

    // 第 1 周固定节点立即解决（Bot 选"对自己最有利"的选项）
    for (const GameEvent* ev : ctx.events->fixedForWeek(1, ctx)) {
        Session::applyChoice(ctx, *ev, defaultResolver().chooseChoice(*ev, ctx));
    }

    int guard = 0;
    int stall = 0;
    int minMoney = ctx.player->wallet().get();
    int lastWeek = ctx.time->week();
    while (!ctx.time->isSemesterOver() && guard++ < 5000) {
        bool consumed = bot->takeTurn(ctx);
        stall = consumed ? 0 : stall + 1;
        if (stall > 8) { // 兜底：策略卡住则强制睡到第二天（昏睡兜底，不计失眠）
            ctx.time->advanceDay();
            ctx.player->energy().restoreFull();
            ctx.locationId = "dorm";
            stall = 0;
        }
        minMoney = std::min(minMoney, ctx.player->wallet().get());
        if (ctx.flags->has("bankrupt")) break; // 炒股爆仓破产（Bot 不炒股，防御性检查）
        if (ctx.time->week() != lastWeek) {    // 周翻转 → 周结算 + 新周固定节点
            lastWeek = ctx.time->week();
            WeekSettlement settle(ctx);
            settle.run();
            if (!ctx.time->isSemesterOver()) {
                for (const GameEvent* ev : ctx.events->fixedForWeek(ctx.time->week(), ctx)) {
                    Session::applyChoice(ctx, *ev, defaultResolver().chooseChoice(*ev, ctx));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    SimGameResult r;
    r.seed = seed;
    r.strategy = strategyName;
    const EndingDef* ending = EndingJudge::judge(ctx);
    r.endingId = ending ? ending->id : "?";
    r.finalCash = ctx.player->wallet().get();
    r.portfolioValue = ctx.trade->estimatePortfolioValue(ctx);
    r.studyScore = ctx.player->studyScore();
    r.maxAffinity = ctx.npcs->maxAffinity();
    r.failed = ctx.flags->has("failed");
    r.minMoney = minMoney;
    return r;
}

std::vector<SimGameResult> SimulationRunner::runGames(const std::string& strategyName,
                                                      std::uint64_t seed0, int count) {
    std::vector<SimGameResult> out;
    out.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(count));
    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        out.push_back(runOne(strategyName, seed0 + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(i)));
    }
    return out;
}

void SimulationRunner::writeCsv(const std::string& path, const std::vector<SimGameResult>& results) {
    std::ofstream f(path, std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
    if (!f) throw std::runtime_error("无法写 CSV: " + path);
    f << "seed,strategy,ending,final_cash,portfolio_value,study_score,max_affinity,failed,min_money\n";
    for (const auto& r : results) {
        f << r.seed << ',' << r.strategy << ',' << r.endingId << ',' << r.finalCash << ','
          << r.portfolioValue << ',' << r.studyScore << ',' << r.maxAffinity << ','
          << (r.failed ? 1 : 0) << ',' << r.minMoney << '\n';
    }
}

void SimulationRunner::printSummary(const std::vector<SimGameResult>& results) {
    // 按策略聚合
    std::map<std::string, std::vector<const SimGameResult*>> byStrategy;
    for (const auto& r : results) byStrategy[r.strategy].push_back(&r);

    std::cout << "\n==== 1000 局模拟摘要（同一套规则，无 UI）====\n";
    std::cout << "策略      局数  校园合伙人  站稳脚跟  账单之外  平均总资产   中位总资产   挂科率  平均平时分  最低现金均值\n";
    for (auto& [name, rs] : byStrategy) {
        std::map<std::string, int> endings;
        long long totalSum = 0;
        std::vector<int> totals;
        int failed = 0;
        long long studySum = 0;
        long long minMoneySum = 0;
        for (const auto* r : rs) {
            endings[r->endingId]++;
            int total = r->finalCash + r->portfolioValue;
            totalSum += total;
            totals.push_back(total);
            studySum += r->studyScore;
            minMoneySum += r->minMoney;
            if (r->failed) ++failed;
        }
        std::sort(totals.begin(), totals.end());
        int median = totals.empty() ? 0 : totals[totals.size() / 2];
        auto rate = [&](const char* id) {
            auto it = endings.find(id);
            double v = it == endings.end() ? 0.0 : static_cast<double>(it->second) / rs.size();
            char buf[16];
            std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%.1f%%", v * 100);
            return std::string(buf);
        };
        char line[256];
        std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%-9s %-5d %-10s %-9s %-9s %-11lld %-12d %-7s %-11lld %lld",
                      name.c_str(), static_cast<int>(rs.size()), rate("partner").c_str(), rate("steady").c_str(),
                      rate("beyond").c_str(), totalSum / static_cast<long long>(rs.size()), median,
                      ([&] { char b[16]; std::snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "%.1f%%",
                              static_cast<double>(failed) / rs.size() * 100); return std::string(b); })().c_str(),
                      studySum / static_cast<long long>(rs.size()),
                      minMoneySum / static_cast<long long>(rs.size()));
        std::cout << line << "\n";
    }
}
````

## `src\stock\StockSystem.cpp`

````cpp
#include "stock/StockSystem.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "core/GameContext.h"
#include "core/Random.h"
#include "player/Player.h"
#include "story/StoryFlags.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

namespace {
std::string fmtPrice(double v) {
    char buf[32];
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%.2f", v);
    return buf;
}
} // namespace

StockSystem::StockSystem(const ConfigManager& cfg, Random& rng)
    : cfg_(cfg),
      rng_(rng),
      stockRng_(rng.next()), // 行情独立随机流：一次播种，不扰动主流
      price_(cfg.balance().stockStartPrice) {
    StockBar first;
    first.o = first.h = first.l = first.c = price_;
    bars_.push_back(first);
}

double StockSystem::price() const { return price_; }
const std::vector<StockBar>& StockSystem::bars() const { return bars_; }
int StockSystem::shares() const { return shares_; }
double StockSystem::avgCost() const { return shares_ > 0 ? costBasis_ / shares_ : 0.0; }
long long StockSystem::loan() const { return loan_; }
int StockSystem::leverage() const { return cfg_.balance().stockLeverages[leverageIndex_]; }
double StockSystem::positionValue() const { return shares_ * price_; }
double StockSystem::equity() const { return positionValue() - static_cast<double>(loan_); }
double StockSystem::unrealizedPnl() const { return positionValue() - costBasis_; }
const std::vector<int>& StockSystem::leverageOptions() const { return cfg_.balance().stockLeverages; }
const std::string& StockSystem::notice() const { return notice_; }

double StockSystem::effectiveFailProb(const GameContext& ctx) const {
    const BalanceDef& b = cfg_.balance();
    // 股力越高，持仓时下一根 K 线收阴概率越低（但永远保底 25%，不会稳赚）
    double p = b.stockFailProb - ctx.player->stockSkill() * b.stockSkillFactor;
    p = std::max(p, b.stockSkillFailFloor);
    if (ctx.player->energy().isLow()) p += b.staminaLowFailBonus;
    return std::min(p, 0.95);
}

void StockSystem::onTick(GameContext& ctx) {
    const BalanceDef& b = cfg_.balance();
    bool holding = shares_ > 0;
    double failP = holding ? effectiveFailProb(ctx) : 0.5;

    // 一根 K 线：开盘 = 前收盘，内部再走若干子步形成高低价，收盘决定下一根开盘
    StockBar bar;
    bar.o = price_;
    bar.h = price_;
    bar.l = price_;
    double p = price_;
    for (int i = 0; i < b.stockIntraSteps; ++i) {
        double drift;
        if (holding) {
            // 大一学生是被收割对象：股力低时 K 线更倾向收阴
            drift = stockRng_.chance(failP)
                        ? -stockRng_.uniform(b.stockVolMin, b.stockVolMax)
                        : +stockRng_.uniform(b.stockVolMin, b.stockVolMax);
        } else {
            drift = (stockRng_.chance(0.5) ? 1.0 : -1.0) *
                    stockRng_.uniform(b.stockVolMin * 0.4, b.stockVolMax * 0.5);
        }
        p = std::clamp(p * (1.0 + drift), 0.5, 999.0);
        bar.h = std::max(bar.h, p);
        bar.l = std::min(bar.l, p);
    }
    if (stockRng_.chance(b.stockCrashProb)) { // 黑天鹅
        p = std::clamp(p * (1.0 - b.stockCrashDrop), 0.5, 999.0);
        bar.h = std::max(bar.h, p);
        bar.l = std::min(bar.l, p);
    }
    if (stockRng_.chance(b.stockBoomProb)) { // 天降横财
        p = std::clamp(p * (1.0 + b.stockBoomRise), 0.5, 999.0);
        bar.h = std::max(bar.h, p);
        bar.l = std::min(bar.l, p);
    }
    step(ctx, p, bar);
}

void StockSystem::applyExternalShock(GameContext& ctx, double mult) {
    if (mult <= 0.0) mult = 0.1;
    double target = std::clamp(price_ * mult, 0.5, 999.0);
    StockBar bar;
    bar.o = price_;
    bar.h = std::max(price_, target);
    bar.l = std::min(price_, target);
    bar.c = target;
    step(ctx, target, bar);
}

void StockSystem::step(GameContext& ctx, double close, const StockBar& bar) {
    notice_.clear();
    price_ = std::round(close * 100.0) / 100.0;
    StockBar fixed = bar;
    fixed.c = price_;
    fixed.h = std::round(std::max(fixed.h, price_) * 100.0) / 100.0;
    fixed.l = std::round(std::min(fixed.l, price_) * 100.0) / 100.0;
    bars_.push_back(fixed);
    if (bars_.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(cfg_.balance().stockBarsKeep))
        bars_.erase(bars_.begin());
    if (shares_ > 0 && equity() <= 0.0) liquidate(ctx); // 爆仓
    ctx.updateBankruptcyFlag();
}

void StockSystem::liquidate(GameContext& ctx) {
    int proceeds = static_cast<int>(std::llround(positionValue()));
    long long debt = loan_;
    ctx.player->wallet().add(proceeds);
    ctx.player->wallet().charge(static_cast<int>(debt)); // 可扣成负数 → 债务
    notice_ = "【爆仓】持仓净值 ≤ 0，强制平仓！回款 ¥" + std::to_string(proceeds) +
              "，偿还融资 ¥" + std::to_string(debt) +
              "（自有本金 ¥" + std::to_string(static_cast<long long>(std::llround(principal_))) +
              " 全部损失）";
    shares_ = 0;
    costBasis_ = 0.0;
    principal_ = 0.0;
    loan_ = 0;
}
TradeResult StockSystem::buy(GameContext& ctx, int yuan) {
    const BalanceDef& b = cfg_.balance();
    if (yuan <= 0) return {false, "投入金额必须为正", 0};
    if (!ctx.player->energy().spend(b.staminaStockCost))
        return {false, "体力不支，盯不动盘了（需要 " + std::to_string(b.staminaStockCost) +
                           " 点体力，回宿舍休息恢复）", 0};

    int lev = leverage();
    int sharesNew = static_cast<int>(std::floor(static_cast<double>(yuan) * lev / price_));
    if (sharesNew <= 0)
        return {false, "金额太小，买不起一手（现价 ¥" + fmtPrice(price_) + "）", 0};

    int cost = static_cast<int>(std::llround(sharesNew * price_));
    int borrowed = std::max(0, cost - yuan); // 融资部分
    int fee = static_cast<int>(std::llround(cost * b.stockFeeRate));
    int outlay = std::min(cost, yuan); // 自有资金实付
    if (!ctx.player->wallet().canAfford(outlay + fee))
        return {false, "现金不够（还要留 ¥" + std::to_string(fee) + " 手续费）", 0};

    ctx.player->wallet().spend(outlay + fee);
    loan_ += borrowed;
    costBasis_ += cost;
    principal_ += outlay + fee; // 自有本金：实付现金 + 买入手续费
    shares_ += sharesNew;
    ctx.updateBankruptcyFlag();
    return {true, "买入 SHU x" + std::to_string(sharesNew) + "（杠杆 x" + std::to_string(lev) +
                      "）实付 ¥" + std::to_string(outlay + fee) +
                      (borrowed > 0 ? "，融资 ¥" + std::to_string(borrowed) : ""), outlay};
}

TradeResult StockSystem::sellAll(GameContext& ctx) {
    if (shares_ <= 0) return {false, "空仓，没什么可卖的", 0};
    const BalanceDef& b = cfg_.balance();
    if (!ctx.player->energy().spend(b.staminaStockCost))
        return {false, "体力不支…（需要 " + std::to_string(b.staminaStockCost) + " 点体力）", 0};

    int heldShares = shares_;
    double proceedsD = positionValue();
    int fee = static_cast<int>(std::llround(proceedsD * b.stockFeeRate));
    int proceeds = std::max(0, static_cast<int>(std::llround(proceedsD)) - fee);
    long long debt = loan_;
    long long principal = static_cast<long long>(std::llround(principal_));

    ctx.player->wallet().add(proceeds);
    if (debt > 0) ctx.player->wallet().charge(static_cast<int>(debt));
    long long net = static_cast<long long>(proceeds) - debt;
    long long pnl = net - principal; // 对自有资金的实现盈亏（含两笔手续费/利息）

    shares_ = 0;
    costBasis_ = 0.0;
    principal_ = 0.0;
    loan_ = 0;
    ctx.updateBankruptcyFlag();

    std::string msg = "清仓 SHU x" + std::to_string(heldShares) + "，回款 ¥" +
                      std::to_string(proceeds) +
                      (debt > 0 ? "，偿还融资 ¥" + std::to_string(debt) : "") +
                      "，净入 ¥" + std::to_string(net);
    if (pnl >= 0) msg += "（盈利 ¥" + std::to_string(pnl) + "）";
    else msg += "（亏损 ¥" + std::to_string(-pnl) + "）";
    return {true, msg, proceeds};
}

TradeResult StockSystem::setLeverage(int lev) {
    const auto& opts = cfg_.balance().stockLeverages;
    auto it = std::find(opts.begin(), opts.end(), lev);
    if (it == opts.end()) return {false, "不支持的杠杆倍数", 0};
    if (shares_ > 0) return {false, "有持仓不能改杠杆，先清仓", 0};
    leverageIndex_ = static_cast<int>(it - opts.begin());
    return {true, "杠杆切换为 x" + std::to_string(lev) + "（融资炒股，爆仓自负）", 0};
}

long long StockSystem::chargeWeeklyInterest() {
    if (loan_ <= 0) return 0;
    long long interest = static_cast<long long>(
        std::llround(loan_ * cfg_.balance().stockLoanWeeklyRate));
    if (interest <= 0) interest = 1;
    loan_ += interest;
    return interest;
}

Json StockSystem::toJson() const {
    Json bars = Json::array();
    for (const auto& b : bars_)
        bars.push_back(Json{{"o", b.o}, {"h", b.h}, {"l", b.l}, {"c", b.c}});
    return Json{
        {"price", price_},
        {"bars", bars},
        {"shares", shares_},
        {"costBasis", costBasis_},
        {"principal", principal_},
        {"loan", loan_},
        {"leverageIndex", leverageIndex_},
        {"rng", stockRng_.toJson()}, // 行情独立随机流状态随存档走
    };
}

void StockSystem::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    price_ = ju::getOr(j, "price", price_);
    bars_.clear();
    if (j.contains("bars") && j["bars"].is_array()) {
        for (const auto& e : j["bars"]) {
            StockBar b;
            b.o = ju::getOr(e, "o", price_);
            b.h = ju::getOr(e, "h", price_);
            b.l = ju::getOr(e, "l", price_);
            b.c = ju::getOr(e, "c", price_);
            if (!bars_.empty()) b.o = bars_.back().c; // 开盘衔接前一根收盘
            bars_.push_back(b);
        }
    } else if (j.contains("history") && j["history"].is_array()) {
        // 旧版存档（纯收盘价序列）→ 合成为平底 K 线，兼容读档
        for (const auto& p : j["history"]) {
            StockBar b;
            double c = p.get<double>();
            b.o = bars_.empty() ? c : bars_.back().c;
            b.h = std::max(b.o, c);
            b.l = std::min(b.o, c);
            b.c = c;
            bars_.push_back(b);
        }
    }
    if (bars_.empty()) {
        StockBar b;
        b.o = b.h = b.l = b.c = price_;
        bars_.push_back(b);
    }
    price_ = bars_.back().c;
    shares_ = ju::getOr(j, "shares", shares_);
    costBasis_ = ju::getOr(j, "costBasis", costBasis_);
    principal_ = ju::getOr(j, "principal", principal_);
    loan_ = ju::getOr(j, "loan", loan_);
    leverageIndex_ = ju::getOr(j, "leverageIndex", leverageIndex_);
    if (leverageIndex_ < 0 ||
        leverageIndex_ >= static_cast<int>(cfg_.balance().stockLeverages.size()))
        leverageIndex_ = 0;
    if (shares_ < 0) shares_ = 0;
    if (loan_ < 0) loan_ = 0;
    if (costBasis_ < 0) costBasis_ = 0.0;
    if (principal_ < 0) principal_ = 0.0;
    if (j.contains("rng")) stockRng_.fromJson(j["rng"]);
}
````

## `src\story\NpcSystem.cpp`

````cpp
#include "story/NpcSystem.h"

#include <algorithm>

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

NpcSystem::NpcSystem(const ConfigManager& cfg) : cfg_(cfg) {
    for (const auto& n : cfg.npcs()) affinity_[n.id] = 0;
}

int NpcSystem::affinity(const std::string& npcId) const {
    auto it = affinity_.find(npcId);
    return it == affinity_.end() ? 0 : it->second;
}

void NpcSystem::addAffinity(const std::string& npcId, int delta) {
    int& v = affinity_[npcId];
    v += delta;
    if (v < 0) v = 0;
    if (v > 100) v = 100;
}

int NpcSystem::maxAffinity() const {
    int best = 0;
    for (const auto& [id, v] : affinity_) best = std::max(best, v);
    return best;
}

Json NpcSystem::toJson() const {
    Json obj = Json::object();
    for (const auto& [id, v] : affinity_) obj[id] = v;
    return Json{{"affinity", obj}};
}

void NpcSystem::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    if (!j.contains("affinity") || !j["affinity"].is_object()) return;
    for (auto it = j["affinity"].begin(); it != j["affinity"].end(); ++it) {
        if (affinity_.count(it.key())) affinity_[it.key()] = it.value().get<int>();
    }
}
````

## `src\time\TimeSystem.cpp`

````cpp
#include "time/TimeSystem.h"

#include "core/ConfigManager.h"
#include "util/JsonUtil.h"

#include <cstdio>

TimeSystem::TimeSystem(const ConfigManager& cfg)
    : weeks_(cfg.balance().weeks) {}

const char* TimeSystem::dayName() const {
    static const char* names[] = {"周一", "周二", "周三", "周四", "周五", "周六", "周日"};
    int d = day_;
    if (d < 0) d = 0;
    if (d > 6) d = 6;
    return names[d];
}

std::string TimeSystem::clockText() const {
    char buf[32];
    if (hour_ < 24) {
        std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%02d:00", hour_);
        return buf;
    }
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "次日 %02d:00", hour_ - 24);
    return buf;
}

bool TimeSystem::advanceDay() {
    bool weekRolled = false;
    lastWeekStudied_ = studiedThisWeek_;
    ++day_;
    if (day_ > 6) {
        day_ = 0;
        ++week_;
        studiedThisWeek_ = false;
        weekRolled = true;
    }
    hour_ = kWakeHour;
    return weekRolled;
}

Json TimeSystem::toJson() const {
    return Json{
        {"week", week_},
        {"day", day_},
        {"hour", hour_},
        {"studiedThisWeek", studiedThisWeek_},
        {"lastWeekStudied", lastWeekStudied_},
    };
}

void TimeSystem::fromJson(const Json& j) {
    week_ = ju::getOr(j, "week", week_);
    day_ = ju::getOr(j, "day", day_);
    hour_ = ju::getOr(j, "hour", hour_);
    studiedThisWeek_ = ju::getOr(j, "studiedThisWeek", studiedThisWeek_);
    lastWeekStudied_ = ju::getOr(j, "lastWeekStudied", lastWeekStudied_);
    if (hour_ < kWakeHour) hour_ = kWakeHour;
    if (hour_ > kCurfewHour) hour_ = kCurfewHour;
}
````

````cpp
#include "ui/Screens.h"

namespace {

std::string money(int v) { return "¥" + std::to_string(v); }

// ================= 标题 =================

void TitleScreen::draw() {
    header("《黄埔江上又一人》 · 校园浮生录");
    line();
    line(console::paint("     别人向东追日出，你向西赶日落。", console::CYAN));
    line("     —— 一个上海书大学生的十二周。");
    line();
    line("[1] 新学期开始    [2] 读取存档    [3] 玩法说明    [0] 退出");
    line();
    line(console::paint("（面向对象程序设计课程项目 · 垂直切片 demo）", console::DIM));
}

void TitleScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    std::string cmd = strutil::toLower(in);
    if (cmd == "1") game_.newGame();
    else if (cmd == "2") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<SaveLoadScreen>(game_, false));
    else if (cmd == "3") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<HelpScreen>(game_));
    else if (cmd == "0") game_.exitApp();
}

// ================= 校园（主界面） =================

void CampusScreen::draw() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    const LocationDef* loc = c->config.location(c->locationId);
    header("第 " + std::to_string(c->time->week()) + " 周 · " + c->time->dayName() +
           " · " + c->time->clockText() +
           " · 体力 " + std::to_string(c->player->energy().get()) + "/" +
           std::to_string(c->player->energy().max()));
    line("现金 " + console::paint(money(c->player->wallet().get()), console::GREEN) +
         " ｜ 平时分 " + std::to_string(c->player->studyScore()) +
         " ｜ 体力 " + std::to_string(c->player->energy().get()) + "/" +
         std::to_string(c->player->energy().max()) +
         " ｜ 背包 " + std::to_string(c->player->inventory().usedSlots()) + "/" +
         std::to_string(c->player->inventory().capacity()) + " 格");
    line("当前地点：" + console::paint(loc ? loc->name : c->locationId, console::YELLOW) +
         "（目标：期末总资产 ≥ " + money(c->config.balance().targetMoney) + "，不挂科）");
    if (!c->rumors.empty()) {
        rule();
        for (const auto& r : c->rumors) line(console::paint(r, console::MAGENTA));
    }
    rule();
    if (!c->lastFeedback.empty()) line(console::paint(c->lastFeedback, console::CYAN));
    rule();
    std::string social = "[5]社交";
    if (loc && !loc->npcId.empty() && c->config.npc(loc->npcId))
        social = "[5]社交(" + c->config.npc(loc->npcId)->name + ")";
    line("[1]移动  [2]市场  [3]打工  [4]学习  " + social + "  [6]睡觉  [x]炒股");
    line("[7]背包  [8]快进到周日结算  [9]存档  [0]读档  [h]帮助  [q]回标题");
}

void CampusScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    std::string cmd = strutil::toLower(in);
    if (cmd == "1") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<TravelScreen>(game_));
    else if (cmd == "2") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<MarketScreen>(game_));
    else if (cmd == "3") game_.performAction(std::make_unique<WorkCommand>());
    else if (cmd == "4") game_.performAction(std::make_unique<StudyCommand>());
    else if (cmd == "5") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<SocialScreen>(game_));
    else if (cmd == "6") game_.performAction(std::make_unique<SleepCommand>());
    else if (cmd == "7") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<InventoryScreen>(game_));
    else if (cmd == "8") game_.finishWeekNow();
    else if (cmd == "9") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<SaveLoadScreen>(game_, true));
    else if (cmd == "0") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<SaveLoadScreen>(game_, false));
    else if (cmd == "x") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<StockScreen>(game_));
    else if (cmd == "h") game_.screens().push(std::make_unique<HelpScreen>(game_));
    else if (cmd == "q") game_.backToTitle();
}

// ================= 移动 =================

void TravelScreen::draw() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    header("去哪儿？（移动耗 " + std::to_string(c->config.balance().moveStamina) +
           " 点体力 · " + std::to_string(c->config.balance().moveHours) + " 小时，不再收路费）");
    int i = 1;
    for (const auto& l : c->config.locations()) {
        std::string prefix = l.id == c->locationId ? "→" : " ";
        line(prefix + " [" + std::to_string(i) + "] " +
             strutil::padRight(l.name, 14) + "  " + console::paint(l.desc, console::DIM));
        ++i;
    }
    rule();
    line("输入编号移动，[q] 返回");
}

void TravelScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    std::string cmd = strutil::toLower(in);
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    if (cmd == "q") {
        game_.screens().pop();
        return;
    }
    int idx = -1;
    try {
        idx = std::stoi(cmd);
    } catch (...) {
        return;
    }
    const auto& locs = c->config.locations();
    if (idx >= 1 && idx <= static_cast<int>(locs.size())) {
        game_.performAction(std::make_unique<MoveCommand>(locs[static_cast<std::size_t>(idx - 1)].id));
        game_.screens().pop();
    }
}

// ================= 市场 =================

void MarketScreen::draw() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    const LocationDef* loc = c->config.location(c->locationId);
    header("市场 · " + (loc ? loc->name : c->locationId) + " · 现金 " + money(c->player->wallet().get()));
    line(strutil::padRight("序号", 6) + strutil::padRight("商品", 14) +
         strutil::padRight("买价", 7) + strutil::padRight("卖价", 7) +
         strutil::padRight("持有", 6) + strutil::padRight("限购余", 7) + "趋势");
    rule();
    auto listings = c->market->listAt(c->locationId);
    int i = 1;
    for (const auto& li : listings) {
        std::string buy = li.canBuy ? std::to_string(li.buyPrice) : "—";
        std::string arrow = c->market->trendArrow(c->locationId, li.goods->id);
        const char* color = arrow[0] == 0xE2 && arrow[1] == 0x96
                                ? (arrow[2] == static_cast<char>(0xB2) ? console::RED : console::GREEN)
                                : console::DIM;
        line(strutil::padRight("[" + std::to_string(i) + "]", 6) +
             strutil::padRight(li.goods->name, 14) +
             strutil::padRight(buy, 7) +
             strutil::padRight(std::to_string(li.sellPrice), 7) +
             strutil::padRight(std::to_string(c->player->inventory().find(li.goods->id)
                                                  ? c->player->inventory().find(li.goods->id)->qty
                                                  : 0), 6) +
             strutil::padRight(li.canBuy ? std::to_string(li.quotaLeft) : "—", 7) +
             console::paint(arrow, color));
        ++i;
    }
    rule();
    line(console::paint(feedback_, console::CYAN));
    line("b <序号> [数量|max] 买入    s <序号> [数量|max] 卖出    q 返回（限购每日刷新）");
}

void MarketScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    std::string cmd = strutil::toLower(in);
    if (cmd == "q" || cmd.empty()) {
        game_.screens().pop();
        return;
    }
    auto tokens = strutil::split(cmd);
    if (tokens.size() < 2) {
        feedback_ = "输入格式：b <序号> [数量|max] 或 s <序号> [数量|max]";
        return;
    }
    bool isBuy = tokens[0] == "b";
    bool isSell = tokens[0] == "s";
    if (!isBuy && !isSell) {
        feedback_ = "未知命令：" + tokens[0];
        return;
    }
    int idx = -1;
    try {
        idx = std::stoi(tokens[1]);
    } catch (...) {
        feedback_ = "序号要是数字";
        return;
    }
    auto listings = c->market->listAt(c->locationId);
    if (idx < 1 || idx > static_cast<int>(listings.size())) {
        feedback_ = "没有这个序号";
        return;
    }
    const Market::Listing& li = listings[static_cast<std::size_t>(idx - 1)];
    const std::string& goodsId = li.goods->id;

    int qty = 1;
    if (tokens.size() >= 3) {
        if (tokens[2] == "max") {
            if (isBuy) {
                int affordable = li.buyPrice > 0 ? c->player->wallet().get() / li.buyPrice : 0;
                int fit = c->player->inventory().freeSlots() / li.goods->slots;
                qty = std::min({li.quotaLeft, affordable, fit});
            } else {
                const Inventory::Item* item = c->player->inventory().find(goodsId);
                qty = item ? item->qty : 0;
            }
        } else {
            try {
                qty = std::stoi(tokens[2]);
            } catch (...) {
                feedback_ = "数量要是数字或 max";
                return;
            }
        }
    }
    TradeResult r = isBuy ? c->trade->buy(*c, goodsId, qty) : c->trade->sell(*c, goodsId, qty);
    feedback_ = r.msg;
    std::string faintMsg;
    if (c->handleFaintAndAdvance(faintMsg)) game_.requestEndWeek(); // 交易跨过周日凌晨
    if (!faintMsg.empty()) feedback_ = faintMsg;
}

// ================= 背包 =================

void InventoryScreen::draw() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    header("背包 · " + std::to_string(c->player->inventory().usedSlots()) + "/" +
           std::to_string(c->player->inventory().capacity()) + " 格");
    if (c->player->inventory().empty()) {
        line("背包空空如也。");
    } else {
        line(strutil::padRight("商品", 14) + strutil::padRight("数量", 6) +
             strutil::padRight("均价", 8) + strutil::padRight("本地卖价", 9) + "浮动盈亏");
        rule();
        for (const auto& item : c->player->inventory().items()) {
            const GoodsDef* g = c->config.goods(item.goodsId);
            if (!g) continue;
            int sellHere = c->market->sellPrice(c->locationId, item.goodsId);
            long long diff = static_cast<long long>(sellHere) * item.qty -
                             static_cast<long long>(item.avgCost) * item.qty;
            std::string diffStr = (diff >= 0 ? "+" : "") + std::to_string(diff);
            line(strutil::padRight(g->name, 14) +
                 strutil::padRight("x" + std::to_string(item.qty), 6) +
                 strutil::padRight(std::to_string(static_cast<int>(item.avgCost)), 8) +
                 strutil::padRight(std::to_string(sellHere), 9) +
                 console::paint(diffStr, diff >= 0 ? console::GREEN : console::RED));
        }
    }
    rule();
    line("[q] 返回（卖出请在市场界面操作）");
}

void InventoryScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    (void)in;
    game_.screens().pop(); // 任意键返回
}

// ================= 炒股（bilibili fx-simple 风：K 线 + 鼠标按钮） =================

namespace {
constexpr int kChartRows = 12; // K 线图行数
constexpr int kChartBars = 18; // 可见 K 线条数
constexpr int kBtnRow1 = 24;   // 按钮行 1（draw 的输出行号）
constexpr int kBtnRow2 = 25;   // 按钮行 2

// 蜡烛图渲染：红涨绿跌（A股习惯），最右侧标注现价
void drawCandles(const std::vector<StockBar>& bars, double lastPrice) {
    if (bars.empty()) {
        std::cout << "（暂无行情）\n";
        return;
    }
    std::size_t start = bars.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(kChartBars)
                            ? bars.size() - static_cast<std::size_t>(kChartBars)
                            : 0;
    double lo = bars[start].l, hi = bars[start].h;
    for (std::size_t i = start; i < bars.size(); ++i) {
        lo = std::min(lo, bars[i].l);
        hi = std::max(hi, bars[i].h);
    }
    if (hi - lo < 1e-9) hi = lo + 1.0;
    double rowSpan = (hi - lo) / kChartRows;
    for (int r = 0; r < kChartRows; ++r) {
        double pTop = hi - (hi - lo) * r / kChartRows;
        double pBot = hi - (hi - lo) * (r + 1) / kChartRows;
        std::string rowStr;
        for (std::size_t i = start; i < bars.size(); ++i) {
            const StockBar& b = bars[i];
            double bodyLo = std::min(b.o, b.c), bodyHi = std::max(b.o, b.c);
            bool up = b.c >= b.o;
            const char* col = up ? console::RED : console::GREEN;
            bool bodyHit;
            if (bodyHi - bodyLo >= rowSpan) bodyHit = bodyLo < pTop && bodyHi > pBot;
            else bodyHit = (bodyLo + bodyHi) / 2 >= pBot && (bodyLo + bodyHi) / 2 < pTop;
            bool wickHit = (b.l < pTop && b.h > pBot) && !bodyHit;
            if (bodyHit) rowStr += console::paint("███", col);
            else if (wickHit) rowStr += console::paint(" │ ", col);
            else rowStr += "   ";
            rowStr += " ";
        }
        if (lastPrice >= pBot && lastPrice <= pTop)
            rowStr += console::paint("← 现价 ¥" + fmtPrice(lastPrice), console::YELLOW);
        std::cout << rowStr << "\n";
    }
}

int bestChoiceForMoney(const GameEvent& ev, const GameContext& ctx) {
    (void)ctx;
    int best = 0;
    long long bv = std::numeric_limits<long long>::min();
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < ev.choices.size(); ++i) {
        long long v = 0;
        for (const auto& e : ev.choices[i].effects) v += e->estimateMoneyDelta();
        if (v > bv) {
            bv = v;
            best = static_cast<int>(i);
        }
    }
    return best;
}

std::string flatten(std::string s) {
    std::size_t pos = 0;
    while ((pos = s.find("\n", pos)) != std::string::npos) {
        s.replace(pos, 1, "；");
        pos += 3;
    }
    return s;
}
} // namespace

// 进入炒股屏：真实控制台 → 接管输入循环（鼠标点击按钮 / 快捷键）；
// 输入被重定向（脚本化回归）→ 回退到键盘行命令模式
void StockScreen::onEnter() {
    if (!console::stdinIsConsole()) {
        lineFallback_ = true;
        return;
    }
    console::InteractiveGuard guard; // 鼠标模式 + 隐藏光标，析构恢复
    bool running = true;
    render();
    while (running) {
        console::ConsoleEvent ev;
        if (!console::pollEvent(100, ev)) continue;
        GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
        if (!c || game_.screens().top() != this) return; // 流程已接管（破产/结局）
        bool changed = false;
        if (ev.mouse && ev.clicked) {
            for (auto& b : buttons_) {
                if (ev.y == b.row && ev.x >= b.c0 && ev.x <= b.c1) {
                    b.action();
                    changed = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (!changed) continue; // 点在空白处不重绘
        } else if (ev.has) { // 键盘
            if (ev.key == 'q' || ev.key == 27) running = false;
            else if (ev.key >= '1' && ev.key <= '6') {
                int idx = ev.key - '1';
                if (idx < static_cast<int>(buttons_.size())) {
                    buttons_[static_cast<std::size_t>(idx)].action();
                    changed = true;
                }
            }
        }
        if (!c || game_.screens().top() != this) return;
        if (changed) render();
        if (c->flags->has("bankrupt")) return; // 爆仓 → 结局流程接管
        if (!c->time->canAct() && !c->time->isSemesterOver()) {
            game_.requestEndWeek(); // 学股市知识耗尽行动点 → 周日结算
            running = false;
        }
    }
    game_.screens().pop(); // 自弹栈（延迟销毁保证安全）
}

void StockScreen::draw() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    const StockSystem* s = c->stock.get();
    header("浦江文旅（SHU）· 炒股模拟器 · 股力 " +
           std::to_string(c->player->stockSkill()) + "/" +
           std::to_string(c->config.balance().stockSkillMax) +
           " · 体力 " + std::to_string(c->player->energy().get()) + "/" +
           std::to_string(c->player->energy().max()));

    // 现价与涨跌（对前一根 K 线收盘）
    const auto& bars = s->bars();
    double prev = bars.size() >= 2 ? bars[bars.size() - 2].c : s->price();
    double pct = prev > 1e-9 ? (s->price() - prev) / prev * 100.0 : 0.0;
    char pctBuf[32];
    std::snprintf(pctBuf, sizeof(pctBuf), "%+.2f%%", pct);
    line(console::paint(std::string("现价 ¥") + fmtPrice(s->price()) + "  " + pctBuf,
                        pct >= 0 ? console::RED : console::GREEN) +
         " ｜ 持仓时收阴概率 " +
         console::paint(std::to_string(static_cast<int>(std::round(s->effectiveFailProb(*c) * 100))) + "%",
                        console::YELLOW) +
         "（股力越高越倾向赚钱，但永不保证）");

    drawCandles(bars, s->price());
    rule();
    if (!s->notice().empty()) line(console::paint(s->notice(), console::RED));
    line("持仓 " + std::to_string(s->shares()) + " 股（成本 ¥" + fmtPrice(s->avgCost()) + "/股）"
         " ｜ 市值 " + money(s->positionValue()) +
         " ｜ 浮动盈亏 " + console::paint((s->unrealizedPnl() >= 0 ? "+" : "") + std::to_string(static_cast<long long>(s->unrealizedPnl())),
                                          s->unrealizedPnl() >= 0 ? console::RED : console::GREEN));
    line("融资负债 " + console::paint(money(static_cast<double>(s->loan())),
                                       s->loan() > 0 ? console::RED : console::DIM) +
         " ｜ 净持仓 " + money(s->equity()) + " ｜ 杠杆 x" + std::to_string(s->leverage()));
    line("现金 " + money(c->player->wallet().get()) +
         " ｜ 破产线：欠债 ≥ " + money(static_cast<double>(c->config.balance().bankruptcyDebt)));
    rule();
    line(console::paint(feedback_.empty() ? "欢迎来到 K 线的世界。" : feedback_, console::CYAN));
    rule();

    // ---- 鼠标按钮（按行列命中） ----
    buttons_.clear();
    const auto& opts = s->leverageOptions();
    int li = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(opts.size()); ++i)
        if (opts[i] == s->leverage()) li = i;
    int nextLev = opts[(li + 1) % opts.size()];
    auto drawButtonRow = [&](int row, const std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::function<void()>>>& defs) {
        int col = 2;
        for (const auto& [label, act] : defs) {
            int w = static_cast<int>(strutil::displayWidth(label));
            buttons_.push_back(Button{row, col, col + w - 1, act});
            std::cout << console::paint(label, console::CYAN);
            col += w + 3;
        }
        std::cout << "\n";
    };
    drawButtonRow(kBtnRow1, {
        {"[1:买入100]", [this] { clickBuy(100); }},
        {"[2:买入1000]", [this] { clickBuy(1000); }},
        {"[3:买入All]", [this] { clickBuy(game_.ctx()->player->wallet().get()); }},
        {"[4:清仓]", [this] { clickSellAll(); }},
    });
    drawButtonRow(kBtnRow2, {
        {"[5:杠杆 x" + std::to_string(s->leverage()) + "→x" + std::to_string(nextLev) + "]",
         [this] { clickCycleLeverage(); }},
        {"[6:学股市知识 -" + std::to_string(c->config.balance().learnStockStaminaCost) + "体力]",
         [this] { clickLearn(); }},
        {"[q:返回]", [this] {}},
    });
    line(console::paint("鼠标点击按钮或按快捷键 1-6；ESC/q 返回（K 线随每次行动与周日结算刷新）",
                        console::DIM));
}

void StockScreen::clickBuy(int yuan) {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    TradeResult r = c->stock->buy(*c, yuan);
    feedback_ = r.msg;
    game_.checkBankruptcy();
}

void StockScreen::clickSellAll() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    TradeResult r = c->stock->sellAll(*c);
    feedback_ = r.msg;
    game_.checkBankruptcy();
}

void StockScreen::clickCycleLeverage() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    const auto& opts = c->stock->leverageOptions();
    int cur = c->stock->leverage();
    int idx = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(opts.size()); ++i)
        if (opts[i] == cur) idx = i;
    TradeResult r = c->stock->setLeverage(opts[(idx + 1) % opts.size()]);
    feedback_ = r.msg;
}

void StockScreen::clickLearn() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    ActionOutcome out = Session::runAction(*c, LearnStockCommand());
    c->lastFeedback = out.result.msg;
    if (out.event) {
        // 炒股屏内触发随机事件：按"对钱最有利"立即解决，结果并入反馈行
        std::string msg = Session::applyChoice(*c, *out.event,
                                               bestChoiceForMoney(*out.event, *c));
        feedback_ = "【" + out.event->name + "】" + flatten(msg);
    } else {
        feedback_ = out.result.msg;
    }
    game_.checkBankruptcy();
    std::string faintMsg;
    if (c->handleFaintAndAdvance(faintMsg)) game_.requestEndWeek(); // 交易跨越周日凌晨
    if (!faintMsg.empty()) feedback_ = faintMsg;
    // 学股市知识 = 2 小时 + 30 体力；昏迷/周结算由 Session/Game 统一处理
}

void StockScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    // 键盘回退模式（输入被重定向时）；交互模式由 onEnter 内循环处理
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    std::string cmd = strutil::toLower(strutil::trim(in));
    if (cmd == "q" || cmd.empty()) {
        game_.screens().pop();
        return;
    }
    auto tokens = strutil::split(cmd);
    if (tokens.empty()) return;
    if (tokens[0] == "b" && tokens.size() >= 2) {
        int yuan = 0;
        if (tokens[1] == "all") yuan = c->player->wallet().get();
        else {
            try {
                yuan = std::stoi(tokens[1]);
            } catch (...) {
                feedback_ = "金额要是数字或 all";
                return;
            }
        }
        clickBuy(yuan);
        return;
    }
    if (tokens[0] == "s") {
        clickSellAll();
        return;
    }
    if (tokens[0] == "l") {
        clickCycleLeverage();
        return;
    }
    if (tokens[0] == "learn") {
        clickLearn(); // 学股市知识 = 2 小时 + 30 体力；昏迷/周结算由 Session/Game 统一处理
        return;
    }
    feedback_ = "命令：b <金额|all> / s all / l 切杠杆 / learn 学股市知识 / q 返回";
}

// ================= 社交（v2：选项池三选一） =================

void SocialScreen::onEnter() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    const LocationDef* loc = c->config.location(c->locationId);
    if (!loc || loc->npcId.empty() || !c->config.npc(loc->npcId)) {
        feedback_ = "这里没有能社交的人。";
        return;
    }
    npcId_ = loc->npcId;
    npcName_ = c->config.npc(npcId_)->name;
    const SocialPoolDef* pool = c->config.socialPool(npcId_);
    if (!pool || pool->positive.empty() || pool->neutral.empty() || pool->negative.empty()) {
        feedback_ = "（" + npcName_ + " 还没有可聊的话题池）";
        return;
    }
    // 从三个池中各抽一条，随机排列展示顺序（不显示分值，玩家凭感觉选）
    options_ = {
        {pool->positive[c->rng.next() % pool->positive.size()], +5},
        {pool->neutral[c->rng.next() % pool->neutral.size()], 0},
        {pool->negative[c->rng.next() % pool->negative.size()], -5},
    };
    for (int i = static_cast<int>(options_.size()) - 1; i > 0; --i) {
        int j = static_cast<int>(c->rng.next() % static_cast<std::uint64_t>(i + 1));
        std::swap(options_[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)], options_[static_cast<std::size_t>(j)]);
    }
}

void SocialScreen::draw() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    header("社交 · " + npcName_ + "（2 小时 · 体力 -" +
           std::to_string(c->config.balance().socialStamina) + "）");
    line("和" + npcName_ + "待在一起，你想做点什么？");
    line();
    if (!options_.empty()) {
        char idx = '1';
        for (const auto& opt : options_) {
            line(std::string("[") + idx + "] " + opt.text);
            ++idx;
        }
    }
    rule();
    line(console::paint(feedback_.empty() ? "输入选项编号执行（每次 2 小时）；[q] 返回" : feedback_,
                        console::CYAN));
    if (options_.empty()) line("[q] 返回");
}

void SocialScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c) return;
    std::string cmd = strutil::toLower(strutil::trim(in));
    if (cmd == "q" || cmd.empty()) {
        game_.screens().pop();
        return;
    }
    if (cmd != "1" && cmd != "2" && cmd != "3") return;
    int pick = cmd[0] - '1';
    if (pick < 0 || pick >= static_cast<int>(options_.size())) return;
    const SocialOption& opt = options_[static_cast<std::size_t>(pick)];
    game_.performAction(std::make_unique<SocialPickCommand>(npcId_, opt.text, opt.delta));
    game_.screens().pop(); // 执行结果显示在校园屏 lastFeedback 上
}

// ================= 事件对话框 =================

void EventDialogScreen::draw() {
    if (!ev_) return;
    header("【" + ev_->name + "】");
    line(ev_->text);
    if (!resolved_) {
        rule();
        int i = 1;
        for (const auto& choice : ev_->choices)
            line("  [" + std::to_string(i++) + "] " + choice.text);
        rule();
        line("输入选项编号：");
    } else {
        rule();
        line(console::paint(resultText_, console::YELLOW));
        rule();
        line("[回车] 继续");
    }
}

void EventDialogScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    if (!c || !ev_) return;
    if (resolved_) {
        game_.screens().pop();
        return;
    }
    int idx = -1;
    try {
        idx = std::stoi(strutil::trim(in));
    } catch (...) {
        return; // 非数字输入忽略
    }
    if (idx >= 1 && idx <= static_cast<int>(ev_->choices.size())) {
        resultText_ = Session::applyChoice(*c, *ev_, idx - 1);
        resolved_ = true;
    }
}

// ================= 周报 =================

void WeeklyReportScreen::draw() {
    const auto& rep = game_.lastReport();
    header("周日结算 · 第 " + std::to_string(rep.week) + " 周结束");
    for (const auto& l : rep.lines) line("  · " + l);
    rule();
    line("现金：" + money(rep.moneyBefore) + " → " +
         console::paint(money(rep.moneyAfter), rep.moneyAfter >= rep.moneyBefore ? console::GREEN : console::RED));
    line("[回车] 进入新的一周");
}

void WeeklyReportScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    (void)in;
    game_.screens().pop();
}

// ================= 存读档 =================

void SaveLoadScreen::draw() {
    header(saveMode_ ? "保存进度" : "读取进度");
    for (const auto& s : game_.saves().listSlots()) {
        std::string label = s.slot == SaveManager::kAutoSlot ? "自动档" : "槽位 " + std::to_string(s.slot);
        line("[" + std::to_string(s.slot) + "] " + strutil::padRight(label, 10) +
             (s.exists ? console::paint(s.summary, console::GREEN) : console::paint("（空）", console::DIM)));
    }
    rule();
    if (!feedback_.empty()) line(console::paint(feedback_, console::CYAN));
    line("[0-3] 选择槽位    [q] 返回");
}

void SaveLoadScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    std::string cmd = strutil::toLower(in);
    if (cmd == "q") {
        game_.screens().pop();
        return;
    }
    if (cmd.size() != 1 || cmd[0] < '0' || cmd[0] > '3') return;
    int slot = cmd[0] - '0';
    std::string err;
    if (saveMode_) {
        feedback_ = game_.saveGame(slot, err) ? "已保存到槽位 " + std::to_string(slot)
                                              : "保存失败: " + err;
    } else {
        if (game_.loadGame(slot, err)) return; // 读档成功：屏幕栈已重置（本屏幕延迟销毁）
        feedback_ = "读档失败: " + err;
    }
}

// ================= 结局 =================

void EndingScreen::draw() {
    GameContext* c = game_.ctx();
    const EndingDef* e = game_.chosenEnding();
    if (!c || !e) return;
    header("结局 · 《" + e->title + "》");
    line();
    line(e->text);
    line();
    rule();
    int portfolio = c->trade->estimatePortfolioValue(*c);
    line("期末总资产（现金+库存估值）：" + money(c->player->wallet().get() + portfolio) +
         "（目标 " + money(c->config.balance().targetMoney) + "）");
    if (c->flags->has("bankrupt"))
        line(console::paint("债务状况：欠债 ¥" + std::to_string(-c->player->wallet().get()) +
                                " —— 征信已破产，本项目提前终止",
                            console::RED));
    line("平时分：" + std::to_string(c->player->studyScore()) +
         (c->flags->has("failed") ? "（挂科）" : "（未挂科）"));
    line("好感：室友 " + std::to_string(c->npcs->affinity("roommate")) +
         " ｜ 学长 " + std::to_string(c->npcs->affinity("senior")) +
         " ｜ 学姐 " + std::to_string(c->npcs->affinity("xuejie")));
    line("本学期交易笔数：" + std::to_string(c->player->ledger().size()));
    rule();
    line("[回车] 回到标题");
}

void EndingScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    (void)in;
    game_.backToTitle();
}

// ================= 帮助 =================

void HelpScreen::draw() {
    header("玩法说明");
    line("· 一学期 16 个教学周 + 第 17、18 周考试周（共 18 周）；每天 8:00 起床，24 小时制。");
    line("· 行动按 小时+体力 计费：移动 1h、学习/社交/学股市 2h、打工 8h；买卖不耗时。");
    line("· 24:00 后仍不睡，熬过凌晨 2 点会昏迷——送校医院扣钱并降低体力上限！");
    line("· 回宿舍[6]睡觉推进到次日 8 点：睡得越久回得越多，睡满 8 小时回满；10% 概率失眠。");
    line("· 学习一次 +3 平时分（考试周 +冲刺）；社交改为三选一，好感 +5/0/-5 看你嘴甜不甜。");
    line("· 炒股：K 线 + 鼠标交易，耗体力不耗时；杠杆爆仓欠债 ≥ ¥3000 → 《破产清算》。");
    line("· 每周生活费自动扣除；期末总资产 ≥ ¥12000 + 不挂科 + 一位好友 → 好结局。");
    line("· 情报来自室友，每周一条，80% 准确 —— 剩下的 20% 就是人生。");
    line();
    line("[任意键] 返回");
}

void HelpScreen::handleInput(const std::string& in) {
    (void)in;
    game_.screens().pop();
}
````
